        function getActiveProfile() { return profiles.find(p => p.id === activeProfileId) || profiles[0]; }
        function updateHeaderProfileUI() {
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            const nameEl = document.getElementById('header-profile-name');
            const dotEl = document.getElementById('header-profile-dot');
            if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = prof.name;
            if (dotEl) dotEl.className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${prof.id === 'me' ? 'bg-brand-400' : 'bg-amber-400'}`;
        }

        function saveState(triggerCloud = true) {
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_exercises', JSON.stringify(exercises));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_active_workout', JSON.stringify(activeWorkout));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_history', JSON.stringify(workoutHistory));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_splits', JSON.stringify(savedSplits));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_supplements', JSON.stringify(supplements));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_user_supp_stacks', JSON.stringify(userSupplementStacks));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_supp_taken_by_date', JSON.stringify(suppTakenByDate));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_profiles', JSON.stringify(profiles));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_active_profile', activeProfileId);
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_expanded_idx', expandedExerciseIndex.toString());
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_nutrition_by_profile', JSON.stringify(nutritionDataByProfile));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_meal_presets', JSON.stringify(mealPresets));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_body_checkins', JSON.stringify(bodyCheckInsByProfile));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_weekly_checkins', JSON.stringify(weeklyCheckInsByProfile));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_biometrics_by_profile', JSON.stringify(biometricsByProfile));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_pre_readiness', JSON.stringify(preWorkoutReadiness));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_community_foods', JSON.stringify(communityFoods));
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_local_updated_at', Date.now().toString());
            if (editingHistoryId) localStorage.setItem('fitlog_editing_history_id', editingHistoryId);
            else localStorage.removeItem('fitlog_editing_history_id');

            if (triggerCloud) scheduleCloudStateSync();
        }

        function initBiometricsUI() {
            const bio = biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] || {};
            const ageEl = document.getElementById('bio-age');
            const heightEl = document.getElementById('bio-height');
            const weightEl = document.getElementById('bio-weight');
            const genderEl = document.getElementById('bio-gender');
            const goalEl = document.getElementById('bio-goal');
            const notesEl = document.getElementById('bio-notes');
            if (ageEl) ageEl.value = bio.age || '';
            if (heightEl) heightEl.value = bio.height || '';
            if (weightEl) weightEl.value = bio.weight || '';
            if (genderEl) genderEl.value = bio.gender || 'male';
            if (goalEl) goalEl.value = bio.goal || 'maintain';
            if (notesEl) notesEl.value = bio.notes || '';
        }

        function saveBiometricsFromUI() {
            biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] = {
                age: document.getElementById('bio-age')?.value.trim() || '',
                height: document.getElementById('bio-height')?.value.trim() || '',
                weight: document.getElementById('bio-weight')?.value.trim() || '',
                gender: document.getElementById('bio-gender')?.value || 'male',
                goal: document.getElementById('bio-goal')?.value || 'maintain',
                notes: document.getElementById('bio-notes')?.value.trim() || ''
            };
            saveState();
        }

        function initPreWorkoutReadinessUI() {
            const eEl = document.getElementById('prep-energy');
            const sEl = document.getElementById('prep-sleep');
            const rEl = document.getElementById('prep-rhr');
            const hEl = document.getElementById('prep-hrv');
            if (eEl) eEl.value = preWorkoutReadiness.energy ?? 8;
            if (sEl) sEl.value = preWorkoutReadiness.sleep ?? 8;
            if (rEl) rEl.value = preWorkoutReadiness.rhr || '';
            if (hEl) hEl.value = preWorkoutReadiness.hrv || '';
            refreshReadinessSummaryBadge();
        }
        function togglePreWorkoutReadiness() {
            const panel = document.getElementById('pre-workout-readiness-panel');
            const arrow = document.getElementById('readiness-toggle-arrow');
            const isHidden = panel.classList.contains('hidden');
            panel.classList.toggle('hidden');
            arrow.textContent = isHidden ? '▲ Skryť' : '▼ Upraviť';
        }
        function updatePreWorkoutReadiness() {
            preWorkoutReadiness = {
                energy: parseInt(document.getElementById('prep-energy').value) || 8,
                sleep: parseInt(document.getElementById('prep-sleep').value) || 8,
                rhr: document.getElementById('prep-rhr').value.trim(),
                hrv: document.getElementById('prep-hrv').value.trim()
            };
            saveState();
            refreshReadinessSummaryBadge();
        }
        function refreshReadinessSummaryBadge() {
            const badge = document.getElementById('readiness-summary-badge');
            const tip = document.getElementById('readiness-coaching-tip');
            if (!badge) return;
            const e = preWorkoutReadiness.energy || 8, s = preWorkoutReadiness.sleep || 8;
            const extras = [];
            if (preWorkoutReadiness.rhr) extras.push(`RHR ${preWorkoutReadiness.rhr} bpm`);
            if (preWorkoutReadiness.hrv) extras.push(`HRV ${preWorkoutReadiness.hrv} ms`);
            badge.textContent = `Energia ${e}/10 • Spánok ${s}/10${extras.length ? ' • ' + extras.join(' • ') : ''}`;
            if (tip) {
                if (e <= 4 || s <= 4) tip.textContent = '⚠️ Nižšia regenerácia – nechaj si väčšiu rezervu (RIR 3–4).';
                else if (e >= 8 && s >= 7) tip.textContent = '🔥 Výborná pripravenosť – ideálny deň na progres (RIR 1–2).';
                else tip.textContent = '✅ Stabilný stav – drž sa plánovaného rozpisu (RIR 2–3).';
            }
        }

        function openWearablesModal() { document.getElementById('wearable-file-preview').classList.add('hidden'); document.getElementById('modal-wearables').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        function closeWearablesModal() { document.getElementById('modal-wearables').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function handleWearableFileImport(event) {
            const file = event.target.files?.[0];
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (e) {
                const parsed = parseWearableFileContent(file.name, String(e.target.result || ''));
                const previewEl = document.getElementById('wearable-file-preview');
                previewEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                previewEl.innerHTML = `<div class="font-extrabold text-brand-400">✓ Načítané zo súboru: ${file.name}</div><div class="text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">Aktivita: <strong>${parsed.title}</strong> • Čas: <strong>${parsed.durationMin} min</strong>${parsed.calories ? ` • Kalórie: <strong>${parsed.calories} kcal</strong>` : ''}${parsed.avgHr ? ` • Priem. tep: <strong>${parsed.avgHr} bpm</strong>` : ''}</div><button type="button" onclick='saveImportedWearableActivity(${JSON.stringify(parsed).replace(/'/g, "&#39;")})' class="w-full py-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs">Zapísať aktivitu do Histórie ✓</button>`;
            };
            reader.readAsText(file);
        }
        function parseWearableFileContent(fileName, content) {
            const lower = fileName.toLowerCase();
            let title = fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''), durationMin = 45, calories = 0, avgHr = 0, steps = 0;
            if (lower.endsWith('.tcx') || lower.endsWith('.xml') || content.includes('<TrainingCenterDatabase')) {
                const sportMatch = content.match(/Sport="([^"]+)"/i);
                if (sportMatch) title = `Hodinky: ${sportMatch[1]}`;
                const timeMatches = [...content.matchAll(/<TotalTimeSeconds>([\d.]+)<\/TotalTimeSeconds>/gi)];
                if (timeMatches.length > 0) durationMin = Math.max(1, Math.round(timeMatches.reduce((a, m) => a + parseFloat(m[1]), 0) / 60));
                const calMatches = [...content.matchAll(/<Calories>(\d+)<\/Calories>/gi)];
                if (calMatches.length > 0) calories = calMatches.reduce((a, m) => a + parseInt(m[1], 10), 0);
                const hrMatches = [...content.matchAll(/<HeartRateBpm>\s*<Value>(\d+)<\/Value>/gi)];
                if (hrMatches.length > 0) avgHr = Math.round(hrMatches.reduce((a, m) => a + parseInt(m[1], 10), 0) / hrMatches.length);
            }
            return { title, durationMin, calories, avgHr, steps };
        }
        function saveImportedWearableActivity(parsed) {
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
            workoutHistory.unshift({ id: 'wearable-' + Date.now(), profileId: prof.id, profileName: prof.name, title: `⌚ ${parsed.title}`, date: dateStr, durationMin: parsed.durationMin || 45, totalVolume: 0, exercises: [] });
            saveState(); renderHistory(); closeWearablesModal(); showToast('Aktivita z hodiniek uložená do Histórie!');
        }
        function parseAndApplyWearableText() {
            const raw = (document.getElementById('wearable-smart-paste').value || '').trim();
            if (!raw) { showToast('Vlož text s údajmi z hodiniek'); return; }
            const extractNum = (regex) => { const m = raw.match(regex); return m ? m[1] : ''; };
            const steps = extractNum(/(?:kroky|steps)[^\d]*(\d+)/i);
            const rhr = extractNum(/(?:tep|rhr|pulse|bpm)[^\d]*(\d+)/i);
            const hrv = extractNum(/(?:hrv)[^\d]*(\d+)/i);
            const sleep = extractNum(/(?:spánok|spanok|sleep)[^\d]*(\d+)/i);
            if (sleep) preWorkoutReadiness.sleep = Math.min(10, Math.max(1, parseInt(sleep, 10)));
            if (rhr) preWorkoutReadiness.rhr = rhr;
            if (hrv) preWorkoutReadiness.hrv = hrv;
            initPreWorkoutReadinessUI();
            const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
            if (!bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId]) bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] = [];
            bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId].unshift({ id: 'ci-smart-' + Date.now(), isoDate: getTodayISO(), date: dateStr, dayType: 'training', weight: '', waist: '', steps: steps || '', rhr: rhr || '', chest: '', thigh: '', arm: '', sleep: preWorkoutReadiness.sleep || 8, fatigue: 7, energy: preWorkoutReadiness.energy || 8, strength: 8, hunger: 6 });
            saveState(); document.getElementById('wearable-smart-paste').value = ''; closeWearablesModal(); showToast('Údaje z hodiniek zapísané ✓');
        }
        function copyAppleShortcutRecipe() {
            copyTextToClipboard(`Ako prepojiť Apple Watch / Apple Health (iOS Skratky):\n1. Otvor na iPhone aplikáciu Skratky -> '+'\n2. Pridaj: 'Nájsť vzorky zdravia' (Počet krokov, Kľudový tep, HRV)\n3. Pridaj 'Text': Kroky: [Kroky], Tep: [Tep], HRV: [HRV]\n4. Pridaj 'Kopírovať do schránky' -> Vlož do MM LAB (⌚ Hodinky).`, 'Návod na Apple Skratku skopírovaný!');
        }
        function exportWorkoutToTCX(workoutId) {
            const w = workoutHistory.find(x => x.id === workoutId);
            if (!w) return;
            const isoStart = new Date().toISOString();
            const durSec = Math.max(300, (w.durationMin || 45) * 60);
            const estKcal = Math.round((w.durationMin || 45) * 7.5);
            const tcx = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<TrainingCenterDatabase xmlns="http://www.garmin.com/xmlschemas/TrainingCenterDatabase/v2"><Activities><Activity Sport="Other"><Id>${isoStart}</Id><Lap StartTime="${isoStart}"><TotalTimeSeconds>${durSec}</TotalTimeSeconds><DistanceMeters>0</DistanceMeters><Calories>${estKcal}</Calories><Intensity>Active</Intensity><TriggerMethod>Manual</TriggerMethod></Lap></Activity></Activities></TrainingCenterDatabase>`;
            const blob = new Blob([tcx], { type: 'application/vnd.garmin.tcx+xml' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = `MM_LAB_${w.date.replace(/\./g, '-')}.tcx`; a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url); showToast('Súbor .TCX pre Garmin / Strava stiahnutý!');
        }

        function playBeepTone(freq = 880, duration = 0.4, doubleBeep = false) {
            try {
                const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
                const ctx = new AudioCtx();
                const trigger = (startTime, f, dur) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator(), gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.type = 'sine'; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(f, startTime);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.28, startTime); gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, startTime + dur);
                    osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(ctx.destination); osc.start(startTime); osc.stop(startTime + dur);
                };
                trigger(ctx.currentTime, freq, duration);
                if (doubleBeep) { trigger(ctx.currentTime + 0.22, freq * 1.25, duration); if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([250, 100, 300]); }
                else if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(90);
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        function switchAppDomain(domain) {
            activeDomain = domain;
            const btns = {
                training: document.getElementById('domain-btn-training'),
                nutrition: document.getElementById('domain-btn-nutrition'),
                supplements: document.getElementById('domain-btn-supplements')
            };
            const bottomNav = document.getElementById('bottom-training-nav');
            Object.keys(btns).forEach(k => {
                if (k === domain) {
                    const activeColor = k === 'supplements' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950';
                    btns[k].className = `py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 ${activeColor} shadow-sm transition`;
                } else {
                    btns[k].className = 'py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                }
            });
            ['workout', 'splits', 'database', 'history', 'nutrition', 'supplements'].forEach(v => document.getElementById(`view-${v}`).classList.add('hidden'));
            if (domain === 'training') { bottomNav.classList.remove('hidden'); switchTab('workout'); }
            else if (domain === 'nutrition') { bottomNav.classList.add('hidden'); document.getElementById('view-nutrition').classList.remove('hidden'); renderNutritionSection(); fetchSharedTrainerMealPlans(false); }
            else { bottomNav.classList.add('hidden'); document.getElementById('view-supplements').classList.remove('hidden'); renderSupplementsSection(); }
        }

        function switchTab(tabName) {
            ['workout', 'splits', 'database', 'history'].forEach(t => {
                document.getElementById(`view-${t}`).classList.add('hidden');
                const btn = document.getElementById(`nav-${t}`);
                btn.classList.remove('text-brand-400', 'bg-brand-500/10');
                btn.classList.add('text-zinc-400');
            });
            document.getElementById(`view-${tabName}`).classList.remove('hidden');
            const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`nav-${tabName}`);
            activeBtn.classList.remove('text-zinc-400');
            activeBtn.classList.add('text-brand-400', 'bg-brand-500/10');
            if (tabName === 'splits') { renderSplits(); fetchSharedTrainerSplits(false); }
            if (tabName === 'database') renderDatabase();
            if (tabName === 'workout') renderActiveWorkout();
            if (tabName === 'history') renderHistory();
        }

        function startRestTimer(seconds = 90) {
            restTimerRemaining = seconds;
            document.getElementById('floating-rest-timer').classList.remove('hidden');
            updateRestTimerDisplay();
            if (restTimerInterval) clearInterval(restTimerInterval);
            restTimerInterval = setInterval(() => {
                restTimerRemaining--;
                if (restTimerRemaining <= 0) { stopRestTimer(); playBeepTone(980, 0.35, true); showToast('⏱ Pauza skončila! Ideš na ďalšiu sériu.'); }
                else { if (restTimerRemaining <= 3) playBeepTone(620, 0.14, false); updateRestTimerDisplay(); }
            }, 1000);
        }
        function adjustRestTimer(deltaSec) { restTimerRemaining = Math.max(5, restTimerRemaining + deltaSec); updateRestTimerDisplay(); }
        function stopRestTimer() { if (restTimerInterval) clearInterval(restTimerInterval); restTimerInterval = null; document.getElementById('floating-rest-timer').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function updateRestTimerDisplay() {
            const m = Math.floor(restTimerRemaining / 60), s = restTimerRemaining % 60;
            document.getElementById('rest-timer-value').textContent = `${m < 10 ? '0' : ''}${m}:${s < 10 ? '0' : ''}${s}`;
        }

        function startLiveSetCountdown(exIdx, setIdx) {
            const ex = activeWorkout[exIdx], set = ex?.sets[setIdx];
            if (!set) return;
            if (liveCountdownState.interval) clearInterval(liveCountdownState.interval);
            liveCountdownState = { exIdx, setIdx, remaining: parseInt(set.duration) || 30, paused: false, interval: null };
            document.getElementById('countdown-ex-name').textContent = ex.name;
            document.getElementById('countdown-set-label').textContent = `Séria ${setIdx + 1} • Cieľ: ${formatDuration(set.duration)}`;
            document.getElementById('countdown-toggle-btn').textContent = 'Pozastaviť';
            updateLiveCountdownUI();
            document.getElementById('modal-live-countdown').classList.remove('hidden');
            liveCountdownState.interval = setInterval(() => {
                if (liveCountdownState.paused) return;
                liveCountdownState.remaining--;
                if (liveCountdownState.remaining <= 0) finishLiveCountdownNow();
                else updateLiveCountdownUI();
            }, 1000);
        }
        function updateLiveCountdownUI() {
            const m = Math.floor(liveCountdownState.remaining / 60), s = liveCountdownState.remaining % 60;
            document.getElementById('countdown-big-time').textContent = `${m < 10 ? '0' : ''}${m}:${s < 10 ? '0' : ''}${s}`;
        }
        function toggleLiveCountdownPause() {
            liveCountdownState.paused = !liveCountdownState.paused;
            document.getElementById('countdown-toggle-btn').textContent = liveCountdownState.paused ? 'Pokračovať' : 'Pozastaviť';
        }
        function finishLiveCountdownNow() {
            if (liveCountdownState.interval) clearInterval(liveCountdownState.interval);
            const { exIdx, setIdx } = liveCountdownState;
            document.getElementById('modal-live-countdown').classList.add('hidden');
            playBeepTone(980, 0.35, true);
            if (exIdx !== null && setIdx !== null && activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx]) {
                activeWorkout[exIdx].sets[setIdx].completed = true;
                saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); startRestTimer(60); showToast('✓ Séria splnená!');
            }
        }
        function closeLiveCountdown() { if (liveCountdownState.interval) clearInterval(liveCountdownState.interval); document.getElementById('modal-live-countdown').classList.add('hidden'); }

        function openWarmupModal(suggestedWeight) {
            document.getElementById('warmup-target-weight').value = parseFloat(suggestedWeight) || 60;
            recalcWarmupSets();
            document.getElementById('modal-warmup').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function recalcWarmupSets() {
            const target = parseFloat(document.getElementById('warmup-target-weight').value) || 0;
            const out = document.getElementById('warmup-sets-output');
            if (target <= 20) { out.innerHTML = `<div class="text-xs text-zinc-300 bg-dark-850 p-3 rounded-xl">Pri ľahkej váhe stačí 1–2 série s vlastnou váhou alebo prázdnou tyčou.</div>`; return; }
            const r25 = val => Math.max(5, Math.round(val / 2.5) * 2.5);
            out.innerHTML = `
                <div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex justify-between text-xs"><span class="text-zinc-400">1. Rozprúdenie (45 %):</span><strong class="text-white">${r25(target * 0.45)} kg × 8–10 opak.</strong></div>
                <div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex justify-between text-xs"><span class="text-zinc-400">2. Aktivácia (65 %):</span><strong class="text-white">${r25(target * 0.65)} kg × 4–5 opak.</strong></div>
                <div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex justify-between text-xs"><span class="text-zinc-400">3. Príprava (85 %):</span><strong class="text-brand-400">${r25(target * 0.85)} kg × 1–2 opak.</strong></div>
            `;
        }

        function openExerciseDetailModal(exerciseId) {
            const ex = exercises.find(e => e.id === exerciseId);
            if (!ex) return;
            const catObj = CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === ex.category);
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-cat').textContent = catObj ? catObj.name : '';
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-title').textContent = ex.name;
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-guide').innerHTML = getExerciseTechniqueSteps(ex.name).map(s => `<div>${s}</div>`).join('');

            const points = [];
            let best1RM = 0;
            const historyRows = [];
            [...workoutHistory].reverse().forEach(w => {
                if ((w.profileId || 'me') !== activeProfileId) return;
                const found = (w.exercises || []).find(item => item.exerciseId === exerciseId);
                if (!found) return;
                let maxVal = 0;
                const setsSummary = (found.sets || []).map(s => {
                    const weight = parseFloat(s.weight) || 0;
                    const reps = parseInt(s.reps) || 1;
                    if (weight > maxVal) maxVal = weight;
                    if (s.mode !== 'time' && weight > 0) best1RM = Math.max(best1RM, Math.round(weight * (1 + reps / 30)));
                    return `${s.weight ? s.weight + 'kg' : 'BW'}×${s.mode === 'time' ? formatDuration(s.duration) : s.reps + 'x'} (RIR ${s.rir})`;
                }).join(' | ');
                if (maxVal > 0) points.push({ date: w.date, val: maxVal });
                historyRows.unshift(`<div class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2 text-[11px]"><div class="font-bold text-brand-400">${w.date}</div><div class="text-zinc-300 mt-0.5">${setsSummary}</div></div>`);
            });

            document.getElementById('ex-detail-1rm-badge').textContent = best1RM > 0 ? `Odhad 1RM: ${best1RM} kg` : 'Bez váhového záznamu';
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-chart').innerHTML = buildSimpleSvgLineChart(points, '#10b981', 'kg');
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-history-rows').innerHTML = historyRows.join('');
            document.getElementById('ex-detail-add-btn').onclick = () => { document.getElementById('modal-exercise-detail').classList.add('hidden'); addExerciseToWorkout(exerciseId); };
            document.getElementById('modal-exercise-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL 2: STRAVA, SKENER ČIAROVÝCH KÓDOV (EAN) & SPOLOČNÁ DATABÁZA
        // =====================================================================
        function getTodayISO() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
        function getYesterdayISO() { const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }

        function getProfileNutrition() {
            if (!nutritionDataByProfile[activeProfileId]) {
                nutritionDataByProfile[activeProfileId] = { targets: { kcal: 2400, p: 160, c: 260, f: 75, water: 2.5 }, logsByDate: {} };
            }
            const today = getTodayISO();
            if (!nutritionDataByProfile[activeProfileId].logsByDate[today]) {
                nutritionDataByProfile[activeProfileId].logsByDate[today] = { meals: [], water: 0 };
            }
            return nutritionDataByProfile[activeProfileId];
        }

        function syncTodayNutritionToCloud(lastMealName = '') {
            const data = getProfileNutrition();
            const todayLog = data.logsByDate[getTodayISO()];
            let totP = 0, totC = 0, totF = 0;
            todayLog.meals.forEach(m => { totP += parseFloat(m.p) || 0; totC += parseFloat(m.c) || 0; totF += parseFloat(m.f) || 0; });
            const kcal = Math.round(totP * 4 + totC * 4 + totF * 9);
            sendCloudTesterLog('nutrition_log', {
                date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK'),
                kcal,
                targetKcal: data.targets.kcal,
                p: Math.round(totP),
                c: Math.round(totC),
                f: Math.round(totF),
                water: todayLog.water || 0,
                mealsCount: todayLog.meals.length,
                lastMeal: lastMealName
            });
        }

        function setNutritionSubTab(sub) {
            activeNutriSubTab = sub;
            const foodBtn = document.getElementById('nutri-tab-food');
            const bodyBtn = document.getElementById('nutri-tab-body');
            const foodView = document.getElementById('nutri-view-food');
            const bodyView = document.getElementById('nutri-view-body');
            if (sub === 'food') {
                foodBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-zinc-800 text-brand-400 border border-brand-500/30 transition';
                bodyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                foodView.classList.remove('hidden'); bodyView.classList.add('hidden');
                renderNutritionFoodView();
            } else {
                bodyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-zinc-800 text-brand-400 border border-brand-500/30 transition';
                foodBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                bodyView.classList.remove('hidden'); foodView.classList.add('hidden');
                initBiometricsUI();
                renderBodyCheckInView();
            }
        }

        function renderNutritionSection() { setNutritionSubTab(activeNutriSubTab); }

        function renderNutritionFoodView() {
            const data = getProfileNutrition();
            const todayLog = data.logsByDate[getTodayISO()];
            const targets = data.targets;

            let totP = 0, totC = 0, totF = 0;
            todayLog.meals.forEach(m => { totP += parseFloat(m.p) || 0; totC += parseFloat(m.c) || 0; totF += parseFloat(m.f) || 0; });
            const totKcal = Math.round(totP * 4 + totC * 4 + totF * 9);
            totP = Math.round(totP); totC = Math.round(totC); totF = Math.round(totF);

            const pct = (cur, tar) => Math.min(100, Math.round((cur / Math.max(1, tar)) * 100));
            document.getElementById('nutri-kcal-current').textContent = totKcal;
            document.getElementById('nutri-kcal-target').textContent = targets.kcal;
            document.getElementById('nutri-kcal-bar').style.width = `${pct(totKcal, targets.kcal)}%`;

            document.getElementById('nutri-p-cur').textContent = totP;
            document.getElementById('nutri-p-tar').textContent = targets.p;
            document.getElementById('nutri-p-bar').style.width = `${pct(totP, targets.p)}%`;

            document.getElementById('nutri-c-cur').textContent = totC;
            document.getElementById('nutri-c-tar').textContent = targets.c;
            document.getElementById('nutri-c-bar').style.width = `${pct(totC, targets.c)}%`;

            document.getElementById('nutri-f-cur').textContent = totF;
            document.getElementById('nutri-f-tar').textContent = targets.f;
            document.getElementById('nutri-f-bar').style.width = `${pct(totF, targets.f)}%`;

            document.getElementById('nutri-water-cur').textContent = (todayLog.water || 0).toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('nutri-water-tar').textContent = targets.water;

            document.getElementById('nutri-presets-list').innerHTML = mealPresets.map(mp => {
                const mkcal = Math.round(mp.p * 4 + mp.c * 4 + mp.f * 9);
                return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">${mp.name}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">${mkcal} kcal • B:${mp.p}g S:${mp.c}g T:${mp.f}g</div></div><button onclick="addPresetMealToToday('${mp.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-500/15 hover:bg-brand-500 text-brand-400 hover:text-zinc-950 border border-brand-500/30 text-[11px] font-extrabold shrink-0 transition">+ Pridať</button></div>`;
            }).join('');

            document.getElementById('nutri-meals-count').textContent = `${todayLog.meals.length} jedál`;
            const todayList = document.getElementById('nutri-today-meals-list');
            if (todayLog.meals.length === 0) {
                todayList.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-4 text-xs text-zinc-500">Zatiaľ si dnes nezapísal žiadne jedlo.</div>`;
            } else {
                todayList.innerHTML = todayLog.meals.map((m, idx) => {
                    const mkcal = Math.round(m.p * 4 + m.c * 4 + m.f * 9);
                    return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">${m.name}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">${mkcal} kcal • B:${m.p}g S:${m.c}g T:${m.f}g</div></div><button onclick="removeMealFromToday(${idx})" class="p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 shrink-0">✕</button></div>`;
                }).join('');
            }
        }

        function copyYesterdayMealsToToday() {
            const data = getProfileNutrition();
            const yMeals = data.logsByDate[getYesterdayISO()]?.meals || [];
            if (yMeals.length === 0) { showToast('Včera nemáš zapísané žiadne jedlá'); return; }
            const todayMeals = data.logsByDate[getTodayISO()].meals;
            yMeals.forEach(m => todayMeals.push({ ...m }));
            saveState(); renderNutritionFoodView(); syncTodayNutritionToCloud('Skopírovaný včerajší jedálniček');
            showToast(`Pridaných ${yMeals.length} jedál zo včera ✓`);
        }

        function addWater(deltaL) {
            const log = getProfileNutrition().logsByDate[getTodayISO()];
            log.water = Math.max(0, Math.round(((log.water || 0) + deltaL) * 100) / 100);
            saveState(); renderNutritionFoodView();
        }

        function addPresetMealToToday(presetId) {
            const mp = mealPresets.find(x => x.id === presetId);
            if (!mp) return;
            getProfileNutrition().logsByDate[getTodayISO()].meals.push({ name: mp.name, p: mp.p, c: mp.c, f: mp.f });
            saveState(); renderNutritionFoodView(); syncTodayNutritionToCloud(mp.name); showToast('Jedlo zapísané do dňa ✓');
        }

        function removeMealFromToday(idx) {
            getProfileNutrition().logsByDate[getTodayISO()].meals.splice(idx, 1);
            saveState(); renderNutritionFoodView();
        }

        function openMacroCalcModal(mode = 'calc') {
            const targets = getProfileNutrition().targets;
            const bio = biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] || {};
            if (bio.age) document.getElementById('calc-age').value = bio.age;
            if (bio.height) document.getElementById('calc-height').value = bio.height;
            if (bio.weight) document.getElementById('calc-weight').value = bio.weight;
            if (bio.gender) document.getElementById('calc-gender').value = bio.gender;

            document.getElementById('manual-p').value = targets.p;
            document.getElementById('manual-c').value = targets.c;
            document.getElementById('manual-f').value = targets.f;
            document.getElementById('manual-water').value = targets.water;
            updateManualKcalPreview();
            setMacroModalMode(mode);
            document.getElementById('modal-macro-calc').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function setMacroModalMode(mode) {
            const calcBtn = document.getElementById('macro-mode-calc-btn'), manBtn = document.getElementById('macro-mode-manual-btn');
            const calcPanel = document.getElementById('macro-panel-calc'), manPanel = document.getElementById('macro-panel-manual');
            if (mode === 'manual') {
                manBtn.className = 'py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 transition';
                calcBtn.className = 'py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                manPanel.classList.remove('hidden'); calcPanel.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                calcBtn.className = 'py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 transition';
                manBtn.className = 'py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                calcPanel.classList.remove('hidden'); manPanel.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        }
        function updateManualKcalPreview() {
            const p = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-p').value) || 0;
            const c = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-c').value) || 0;
            const f = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-f').value) || 0;
            document.getElementById('manual-kcal-preview').textContent = `${Math.round(p * 4 + c * 4 + f * 9)} kcal`;
        }
        function applyManualMacros() {
            const p = Math.max(0, Math.round(parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-p').value) || 0));
            const c = Math.max(0, Math.round(parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-c').value) || 0));
            const f = Math.max(0, Math.round(parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-f').value) || 0));
            const water = Math.max(0.5, Math.round((parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-water').value) || 2.5) * 10) / 10);
            const kcal = Math.round(p * 4 + c * 4 + f * 9);
            getProfileNutrition().targets = { kcal, p, c, f, water };
            saveState(); document.getElementById('modal-macro-calc').classList.add('hidden'); renderNutritionFoodView(); showToast(`Vlastné makrá uložené: ${kcal} kcal`);
        }
        function applyCalculatedMacros() {
            const gender = document.getElementById('calc-gender').value;
            const age = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-age').value) || 30;
            const height = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-height').value) || 175;
            const weight = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-weight').value) || 75;
            const activity = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-activity').value) || 1.55;
            const goal = document.getElementById('calc-goal').value;

            biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] = {
                ...(biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] || {}),
                gender, age: String(age), height: String(height), weight: String(weight), goal
            };

            let bmr = 10 * weight + 6.25 * height - 5 * age + (gender === 'male' ? 5 : -161);
            let tdee = bmr * activity;
            if (goal === 'cut') tdee *= 0.82;
            if (goal === 'bulk') tdee *= 1.12;
            const kcal = Math.round(tdee);
            const p = Math.round(weight * 1.9);
            const f = Math.round(weight * 0.85);
            const c = Math.max(50, Math.round((kcal - p * 4 - f * 9) / 4));
            const water = Math.round(weight * 0.035 * 10) / 10;
            getProfileNutrition().targets = { kcal, p, c, f, water };
            saveState(); document.getElementById('modal-macro-calc').classList.add('hidden'); renderNutritionFoodView(); showToast(`Cieľ nastavený: ${kcal} kcal / deň`);
        }

        function openAddMealModal() { document.getElementById('modal-add-meal').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        function saveCustomMealEntry() {
            const name = document.getElementById('meal-name').value.trim() || 'Vlastné jedlo';
            const p = parseInt(document.getElementById('meal-p').value) || 0;
            const c = parseInt(document.getElementById('meal-c').value) || 0;
            const f = parseInt(document.getElementById('meal-f').value) || 0;
            getProfileNutrition().logsByDate[getTodayISO()].meals.push({ name, p, c, f });
            if (document.getElementById('meal-save-preset').checked) mealPresets.unshift({ id: 'mp-' + Date.now(), name, p, c, f });
            saveState(); document.getElementById('meal-name').value = ''; document.getElementById('modal-add-meal').classList.add('hidden');
            renderNutritionFoodView(); syncTodayNutritionToCloud(name); showToast('Jedlo pridané');
        }

        // SKLADAČKA JEDLA + SKENER ČIAROVÝCH KÓDOV (EAN) + COMMUNITY FOOD CATALOG
        let selectedFoodCat = 'all', draftMealIngredients = [];
        let html5QrScannerInstance = null;
        let pendingNewEanCode = '';

        async function fetchCommunityFoodCatalog() {
            try {
                const rows = await supabaseFetch('food_catalog?select=barcode,name,p_100g,c_100g,f_100g&order=created_at.desc&limit=300');
                if (Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length > 0) {
                    communityFoods = rows.map(r => ({
                        id: `ean-${r.barcode}`,
                        barcode: r.barcode,
                        name: `${r.name} [EAN]`,
                        cat: 'scanned',
                        p: parseFloat(r.p_100g) || 0,
                        c: parseFloat(r.c_100g) || 0,
                        f: parseFloat(r.f_100g) || 0
                    }));
                    localStorage.setItem('fitlog_community_foods', JSON.stringify(communityFoods));
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        function openMealBuilderModal() {
            selectedFoodCat = 'all'; draftMealIngredients = [];
            document.getElementById('mb-meal-title').value = '';
            document.getElementById('ean-manual-input').value = '';
            document.getElementById('ean-status-box').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('ean-new-product-form').classList.add('hidden');
            const searchEl = document.getElementById('mb-food-search');
            if (searchEl) searchEl.value = '';
            fetchCommunityFoodCatalog().then(() => {
                renderMealBuilderFoodPills();
                renderMealBuilderFoodSelect();
            });
            renderMealBuilderFoodPills();
            renderMealBuilderFoodSelect();
            renderMealBuilderIngredients();
            document.getElementById('modal-meal-builder').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeMealBuilderModal() {
            stopBarcodeCameraScanner();
            document.getElementById('modal-meal-builder').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        async function toggleBarcodeCameraScanner() {
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('ean-camera-wrapper');
            if (!wrapper.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                await stopBarcodeCameraScanner();
                return;
            }
            if (typeof Html5Qrcode === 'undefined') {
                showToast('Skener kamery sa nepodarilo načítať – zadaj číslo EAN ručne');
                return;
            }
            wrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
            try {
                html5QrScannerInstance = new Html5Qrcode('ean-qr-reader');
                await html5QrScannerInstance.start(
                    { facingMode: 'environment' },
                    { fps: 10, qrbox: { width: 240, height: 120 } },
                    async (decodedText) => {
                        const cleanEan = decodedText.replace(/\D/g, '');
                        if (cleanEan.length >= 8) {
                            playBeepTone(1040, 0.2, false);
                            document.getElementById('ean-manual-input').value = cleanEan;
                            await stopBarcodeCameraScanner();
                            await lookupBarcodeProduct();
                        }
                    },
                    () => {}
                );
            } catch (err) {
                wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
                showToast('Kamera nie je dostupná (povoľ prístup v prehliadači alebo zadaj EAN ručne)');
            }
        }

        async function stopBarcodeCameraScanner() {
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('ean-camera-wrapper');
            if (wrapper) wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
            if (html5QrScannerInstance) {
                try { await html5QrScannerInstance.stop(); } catch (e) {}
                try { html5QrScannerInstance.clear(); } catch (e) {}
                html5QrScannerInstance = null;
            }
        }

        async function lookupBarcodeProduct() {
            const ean = (document.getElementById('ean-manual-input').value || '').replace(/\D/g, '').trim();
            const statusBox = document.getElementById('ean-status-box');
            const newForm = document.getElementById('ean-new-product-form');
            if (ean.length < 8) { showToast('Zadaj platný čiarový kód (min. 8 číslic)'); return; }

            statusBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            statusBox.className = 'text-xs rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-sky-500/15 border border-sky-500/35 text-sky-300';
            statusBox.textContent = `🔍 Hľadám produkt EAN ${ean}...`;
            newForm.classList.add('hidden');

            try {
                // 1. KROK: Vlastná spoločná databáza v Supabase (food_catalog)
                const localRows = await supabaseFetch(`food_catalog?barcode=eq.${encodeURIComponent(ean)}&select=barcode,name,p_100g,c_100g,f_100g`).catch(() => []);
                if (localRows && localRows.length > 0) {
                    const item = localRows[0];
                    addScannedFoodDirectlyToPlate(item.name, parseFloat(item.p_100g) || 0, parseFloat(item.c_100g) || 0, parseFloat(item.f_100g) || 0, ean);
                    statusBox.className = 'text-xs rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-brand-500/15 border border-brand-500/40 text-brand-300';
                    statusBox.textContent = `✓ Nájdené v MM LAB databáze: ${item.name} (pridané 100 g na tanier)`;
                    return;
                }

                // 2. KROK: Bezplatná svetová databáza Open Food Facts API
                const offRes = await fetch(`https://world.openfoodfacts.org/api/v2/product/${encodeURIComponent(ean)}.json`);
                const offData = await offRes.json();
                if (offData && offData.status === 1 && offData.product) {
                    const prod = offData.product;
                    const nutr = prod.nutriments || {};
                    const prodName = (prod.product_name_sk || prod.product_name_cs || prod.product_name || 'Produkt z obchodu').trim();
                    const brand = (prod.brands || '').split(',')[0].trim();
                    const fullName = brand && !prodName.toLowerCase().includes(brand.toLowerCase()) ? `${brand} – ${prodName}` : prodName;
                    const p100 = Math.round((parseFloat(nutr.proteins_100g) || 0) * 10) / 10;
                    const c100 = Math.round((parseFloat(nutr.carbohydrates_100g) || 0) * 10) / 10;
                    const f100 = Math.round((parseFloat(nutr.fat_100g) || 0) * 10) / 10;

                    // Uložíme do spoločného food_catalog v Supabase pre všetkých ostatných testerov/klientov
                    supabaseFetch('food_catalog', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Prefer': 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            barcode: ean,
                            name: fullName,
                            p_100g: p100,
                            c_100g: c100,
                            f_100g: f100,
                            added_by: currentTesterSession?.code || 'ANON'
                        })
                    }).catch(() => {});

                    addScannedFoodDirectlyToPlate(fullName, p100, c100, f100, ean);
                    statusBox.className = 'text-xs rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-brand-500/15 border border-brand-500/40 text-brand-300';
                    statusBox.textContent = `✓ Načítané z Open Food Facts: ${fullName} (pridané 100 g na tanier)`;
                    return;
                }

                // 3. KROK: Fallback formulár pre zadanie z etikety
                pendingNewEanCode = ean;
                document.getElementById('ean-new-code-label').textContent = ean;
                document.getElementById('ean-new-name').value = '';
                document.getElementById('ean-new-p').value = '';
                document.getElementById('ean-new-c').value = '';
                document.getElementById('ean-new-f').value = '';
                statusBox.classList.add('hidden');
                newForm.classList.remove('hidden');
            } catch (e) {
                pendingNewEanCode = ean;
                document.getElementById('ean-new-code-label').textContent = ean;
                statusBox.classList.add('hidden');
                newForm.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        }

        function addScannedFoodDirectlyToPlate(name, p100, c100, f100, ean) {
            const grams = parseFloat(document.getElementById('mb-food-grams').value) || 100;
            const mult = grams / 100;
            draftMealIngredients.push({
                name,
                grams,
                p: Math.round(p100 * mult * 10) / 10,
                c: Math.round(c100 * mult * 10) / 10,
                f: Math.round(f100 * mult * 10) / 10
            });
            if (!communityFoods.some(f => f.barcode === ean)) {
                communityFoods.unshift({ id: `ean-${ean}`, barcode: ean, name: `${name} [EAN]`, cat: 'scanned', p: p100, c: c100, f: f100 });
                saveState();
                renderMealBuilderFoodSelect();
            }
            renderMealBuilderIngredients();
        }

        async function saveNewScannedFoodToCatalog() {
            const name = document.getElementById('ean-new-name').value.trim();
            if (!name || !pendingNewEanCode) { showToast('Zadaj názov produktu'); return; }
            const p100 = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ean-new-p').value) || 0;
            const c100 = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ean-new-c').value) || 0;
            const f100 = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ean-new-f').value) || 0;

            try {
                await supabaseFetch('food_catalog', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Prefer': 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        barcode: pendingNewEanCode,
                        name,
                        p_100g: p100,
                        c_100g: c100,
                        f_100g: f100,
                        added_by: currentTesterSession?.code || 'ANON'
                    })
                });
            } catch (e) {}

            addScannedFoodDirectlyToPlate(name, p100, c100, f100, pendingNewEanCode);
            document.getElementById('ean-new-product-form').classList.add('hidden');
            showToast(`Produkt "${name}" uložený pre všetkých & pridaný ✓`);
        }

        function renderMealBuilderFoodPills() {
            document.getElementById('mb-food-cat-pills').innerHTML = FOOD_CATEGORIES.map(c => `<button type="button" onclick="selectedFoodCat='${c.id}'; renderMealBuilderFoodPills(); renderMealBuilderFoodSelect();" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition ${selectedFoodCat === c.id ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-900 text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-700'}">${c.name}</button>`).join('');
        }
        function renderMealBuilderFoodSelect() {
            const q = (document.getElementById('mb-food-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const allFoods = getAllFoodsList();
            const list = allFoods.filter(f => (selectedFoodCat === 'all' || f.cat === selectedFoodCat) && (!q || f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
            const sel = document.getElementById('mb-food-select');
            if (list.length === 0) { sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">Žiadna potravina sa nenašla</option>`; return; }
            sel.innerHTML = list.map(f => {
                const kcal = Math.round(f.p * 4 + f.c * 4 + f.f * 9);
                return `<option value="${f.id}">${f.name} — ${kcal} kcal (B:${f.p} S:${f.c} T:${f.f})</option>`;
            }).join('');
        }
        function addIngredientToMealBuilder() {
            const foodId = document.getElementById('mb-food-select').value;
            const grams = parseFloat(document.getElementById('mb-food-grams').value) || 0;
            const food = getAllFoodsList().find(f => f.id === foodId);
            if (!food || grams <= 0) return;
            const mult = grams / 100;
            draftMealIngredients.push({ name: food.name, grams, p: Math.round(food.p * mult * 10) / 10, c: Math.round(food.c * mult * 10) / 10, f: Math.round(food.f * mult * 10) / 10 });
            renderMealBuilderIngredients();
        }
        function removeIngredientFromMealBuilder(idx) { draftMealIngredients.splice(idx, 1); renderMealBuilderIngredients(); }
        function renderMealBuilderIngredients() {
            const container = document.getElementById('mb-ingredients-list');
            let p = 0, c = 0, f = 0;
            draftMealIngredients.forEach(i => { p += i.p; c += i.c; f += i.f; });
            document.getElementById('mb-total-kcal').textContent = `${Math.round(p * 4 + c * 4 + f * 9)} kcal`;
            document.getElementById('mb-total-p').textContent = Math.round(p);
            document.getElementById('mb-total-c').textContent = Math.round(c);
            document.getElementById('mb-total-f').textContent = Math.round(f);
            if (draftMealIngredients.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-3 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-xl">Naskenuj EAN kód alebo pridaj surovinu vyššie.</div>`; return; }
            container.innerHTML = draftMealIngredients.map((item, idx) => `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-bold text-white truncate">${item.name} (${item.grams} g)</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">B:${item.p}g • S:${item.c}g • T:${item.f}g</div></div><button type="button" onclick="removeIngredientFromMealBuilder(${idx})" class="text-rose-400 p-1">✕</button></div>`).join('');
        }
        function saveBuiltMealToToday() {
            if (draftMealIngredients.length === 0) { showToast('Pridaj najprv aspoň jednu surovinu'); return; }
            let p = 0, c = 0, f = 0;
            draftMealIngredients.forEach(i => { p += i.p; c += i.c; f += i.f; });
            const autoName = draftMealIngredients.map(i => `${i.name.split('(')[0].replace('[EAN]', '').trim()} ${i.grams}g`).join(' + ');
            const name = document.getElementById('mb-meal-title').value.trim() || autoName;
            const mealObj = { name, p: Math.round(p), c: Math.round(c), f: Math.round(f) };
            getProfileNutrition().logsByDate[getTodayISO()].meals.push(mealObj);
            if (document.getElementById('mb-save-as-preset').checked) mealPresets.unshift({ id: 'mp-' + Date.now(), ...mealObj });
            saveState(); closeMealBuilderModal();
            renderNutritionFoodView(); syncTodayNutritionToCloud(name); showToast('Jedlo vyskladané a uložené ✓');
        }

        const DAY_TYPE_LABELS = { training: '🏋️ Tréningový deň', run: '🏃 Beh / Kondícia', rest: '🧘 Oddychový deň' };
        function setBodyCheckInMode(mode) {
            activeBodyCheckInMode = mode;
            const dailyBtn = document.getElementById('body-mode-daily-btn'), weeklyBtn = document.getElementById('body-mode-weekly-btn');
            const dailyPanel = document.getElementById('body-panel-daily'), weeklyPanel = document.getElementById('body-panel-weekly');
            if (mode === 'weekly') {
                weeklyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 transition';
                dailyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                weeklyPanel.classList.remove('hidden'); dailyPanel.classList.add('hidden');
                renderWeeklyCheckInView();
            } else {
                dailyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950 transition';
                weeklyBtn.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                dailyPanel.classList.remove('hidden'); weeklyPanel.classList.add('hidden');
                renderDailyCheckInHistory();
            }
        }
        function saveBodyCheckIn() {
            const weight = document.getElementById('checkin-weight').value.trim();
            const dayType = document.getElementById('checkin-day-type').value;
            const sleep = document.getElementById('checkin-sleep').value;
            const fatigue = document.getElementById('checkin-fatigue').value;
            const energy = document.getElementById('checkin-energy').value;
            const strength = document.getElementById('checkin-strength').value;
            const hunger = document.getElementById('checkin-hunger').value;
            const steps = document.getElementById('checkin-steps')?.value.trim() || '';
            const rhr = document.getElementById('checkin-rhr')?.value.trim() || '';
            if (!bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId]) bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] = [];
            const isoDate = getTodayISO();
            const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
            const existingIdx = bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId].findIndex(item => item.isoDate === isoDate);
            const entry = { id: existingIdx >= 0 ? bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId][existingIdx].id : 'ci-' + Date.now(), isoDate, date: dateStr, dayType, weight, sleep, fatigue, energy, strength, hunger, steps, rhr };
            if (existingIdx >= 0) bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId][existingIdx] = entry;
            else bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId].unshift(entry);

            if (weight) {
                biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] = { ...(biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] || {}), weight };
                initBiometricsUI();
            }
            preWorkoutReadiness.energy = parseInt(energy) || 8;
            preWorkoutReadiness.sleep = parseInt(sleep) || 8;
            if (rhr) preWorkoutReadiness.rhr = rhr;
            initPreWorkoutReadinessUI();
            saveState(); renderDailyCheckInHistory(); sendCloudTesterLog('morning_checkin', entry); showToast('Ranný dotazník uložený ✓');
        }
        function shareCheckInReport(ciId) {
            const ci = (bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] || []).find(x => x.id === ciId);
            if (!ci) return;
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            copyTextToClipboard(`MM LAB – Ranný dotazník (${prof.name})\n📅 Dátum: ${ci.date} | ${DAY_TYPE_LABELS[ci.dayType] || '🏋️ Tréningový deň'}\n☀️ DNES RÁNO:\n• Váha: ${ci.weight || '—'} kg | Spánok: ${ci.sleep}/10\n• Sviežosť: ${ci.fatigue ?? '—'}/10 | Sila: ${ci.strength ?? '—'}/10\n⏮️ PREDOŠLÝ DEŇ:\n• Energia včera: ${ci.energy}/10 | Hlad včera: ${ci.hunger ?? '—'}/10\n• Kroky včera: ${ci.steps || '—'} | Tep včera: ${ci.rhr || '—'} bpm`, 'Ranný dotazník skopírovaný!');
        }
        function renderBodyCheckInView() { setBodyCheckInMode(activeBodyCheckInMode); }
        function renderDailyCheckInHistory() {
            const container = document.getElementById('body-checkin-history');
            const list = bodyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] || [];
            const last7 = list.slice(0, 7);
            const avgE = last7.length ? (last7.reduce((a, x) => a + (parseFloat(x.energy) || 0), 0) / last7.length).toFixed(1) : '—';
            const avgS = last7.length ? (last7.reduce((a, x) => a + (parseFloat(x.sleep) || 0), 0) / last7.length).toFixed(1) : '—';
            const withW = list.find(x => x.weight);
            document.getElementById('weekly-avg-energy').textContent = `${avgE} / 10`;
            document.getElementById('weekly-avg-sleep').textContent = `${avgS} / 10`;
            document.getElementById('weekly-last-weight').textContent = withW ? `${withW.weight} kg` : '— kg';
            if (list.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-6 text-xs text-zinc-500 bg-dark-900/50 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl">Zatiaľ žiadny uložený ranný dotazník.</div>`; return; }
            container.innerHTML = list.map(ci => {
                const dayBadge = DAY_TYPE_LABELS[ci.dayType] || '🏋️ Tréningový deň';
                const isRest = ci.dayType === 'rest';
                return `<div class="bg-dark-900 border ${isRest ? 'border-purple-500/35' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-2xl p-3.5 space-y-2.5"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap"><span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-brand-400">📅 ${ci.date}</span><span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${isRest ? 'bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/30' : 'bg-dark-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-700'}">${dayBadge}</span></div><button onclick="shareCheckInReport('${ci.id}')" class="text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-dark-800 hover:bg-dark-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 shrink-0">📤 Zdielať</button></div><div class="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5 text-center text-[11px]"><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Ranná váha</span><strong class="text-white">${ci.weight || '—'} kg</strong></div><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Spánok</span><strong class="text-brand-400">${ci.sleep}/10</strong></div><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Sviežosť</span><strong class="text-sky-400">${ci.fatigue ?? '—'}/10</strong></div><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Sila ráno</span><strong class="text-purple-400">${ci.strength ?? '—'}/10</strong></div><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Energia včera</span><strong class="text-amber-400">${ci.energy}/10</strong></div><div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Hlad včera</span><strong class="text-rose-400">${ci.hunger ?? '—'}/10</strong></div></div>${(ci.steps || ci.rhr) ? `<div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">⏮️ Predošlý deň – Kroky: <strong class="text-white">${ci.steps || '—'}</strong> • Srdcový tep: <strong class="text-rose-300">${ci.rhr || '—'} bpm</strong></div>` : ''}</div>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        const MEASURE_GUIDES = {
            waist: '<strong>Pás:</strong> Meraj ráno nalačno, v stoji s uvoľneným bruchom (bez zaťahovania), vodorovne presne cez úroveň pupka.',
            hips: '<strong>Boky:</strong> Meraj v najširšom mieste zadku a bokov, chodidlá pri sebe, krajčírsky meter drž vodorovne.',
            chest: '<strong>Hrudník:</strong> Meraj vodorovne cez najširšiu časť hrudníka (cez bradavky) po pokojnom výdychu, ruky voľne pri tele.',
            thigh: '<strong>Stehno:</strong> Meraj v stoji, váha rovnomerne na oboch nohách, v najširšom mieste stehna (cca 15–20 cm nad jabĺčkom).',
            arm: '<strong>Ruka (Biceps):</strong> Meraj v najširšom mieste nadlaktia (stred bicepsu) v miernom napnutí v 90° uhle, vždy na tej istej ruke.'
        };
        let activeMeasureGuideKey = null;
        function toggleMeasureGuide(key) {
            const box = document.getElementById('measure-guide-box');
            if (!box) return;
            if (activeMeasureGuideKey === key && !box.classList.contains('hidden')) { box.classList.add('hidden'); activeMeasureGuideKey = null; return; }
            activeMeasureGuideKey = key; box.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (key === 'all') box.innerHTML = `<div class="flex justify-between items-center font-extrabold text-sky-300 mb-1"><span>📏 Presný postup merania (vždy ráno nalačno):</span><button type="button" onclick="toggleMeasureGuide('${key}')" class="text-zinc-400">✕</button></div>` + Object.values(MEASURE_GUIDES).map(t => `<div>• ${t}</div>`).join('');
            else box.innerHTML = `<div class="flex justify-between items-start gap-2"><div>${MEASURE_GUIDES[key] || ''}</div><button type="button" onclick="toggleMeasureGuide('${key}')" class="text-zinc-400 shrink-0">✕</button></div>`;
        }

        function saveWeeklyCheckIn() {
            const wVal = document.getElementById('wk-weight').value.trim();
            const entry = {
                id: 'wk-' + Date.now(),
                date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' }),
                weight: wVal,
                waist: document.getElementById('wk-waist').value.trim(),
                hips: document.getElementById('wk-hips').value.trim(),
                chest: document.getElementById('wk-chest').value.trim(),
                thigh: document.getElementById('wk-thigh').value.trim(),
                arm: document.getElementById('wk-arm').value.trim(),
                energy: document.getElementById('wk-energy').value,
                hunger: document.getElementById('wk-hunger').value,
                sleep: document.getElementById('wk-sleep').value,
                recovery: document.getElementById('wk-recovery').value,
                adherence: document.getElementById('wk-adherence').value,
                stress: document.getElementById('wk-stress').value,
                note: document.getElementById('wk-note').value.trim()
            };
            if (!weeklyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId]) weeklyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] = [];
            weeklyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId].unshift(entry);
            if (wVal) { biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] = { ...(biometricsByProfile[activeProfileId] || {}), weight: wVal }; initBiometricsUI(); }
            document.getElementById('wk-note').value = '';
            saveState(); renderWeeklyCheckInView(); sendCloudTesterLog('weekly_checkin', entry); showToast('Týždenný Check-in uložený ✓');
        }
        function shareWeeklyCheckInReport(wkId) {
            const w = (weeklyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] || []).find(x => x.id === wkId);
            if (!w) return;
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            copyTextToClipboard(`MM LAB – Týždenný Check-in (${prof.name})\n📅 Dátum: ${w.date}\n📐 TELESNÉ MIERY:\n• Váha: ${w.weight || '—'} kg | Pás: ${w.waist || '—'} cm | Boky: ${w.hips || '—'} cm\n• Hrudník: ${w.chest || '—'} cm | Stehno: ${w.thigh || '—'} cm | Ruka: ${w.arm || '—'} cm\n📊 HODNOTENIE TÝŽDŇA:\n• Energia: ${w.energy}/10 | Hlad: ${w.hunger}/10 | Spánok: ${w.sleep}/10\n• Regenerácia: ${w.recovery}/10 | Dodržanie plánu: ${w.adherence}/10 | Stres: ${w.stress}/10${w.note ? `\n📝 Poznámka: ${w.note}` : ''}`, 'Týždenný Check-in skopírovaný!');
        }
        function renderWeeklyCheckInView() {
            const container = document.getElementById('weekly-checkin-history');
            const list = weeklyCheckInsByProfile[activeProfileId] || [];
            if (list.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-6 text-xs text-zinc-500 bg-dark-900/50 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl">Zatiaľ žiadny uložený týždenný Check-in.</div>`; return; }
            container.innerHTML = list.map(w => `
                <div class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-brand-400">📏 Týždenný Check-in • ${w.date}</span><button onclick="shareWeeklyCheckInReport('${w.id}')" class="text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-brand-500/15 text-brand-400 border border-brand-500/30">📤 Report</button></div>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5 text-center text-[11px]">
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Váha</span><strong class="text-white">${w.weight || '—'} kg</strong></div>
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Pás</span><strong class="text-white">${w.waist || '—'} cm</strong></div>
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Boky</span><strong class="text-white">${w.hips || '—'} cm</strong></div>
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Hrudník</span><strong class="text-white">${w.chest || '—'} cm</strong></div>
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Stehno</span><strong class="text-white">${w.thigh || '—'} cm</strong></div>
                        <div class="bg-dark-850 p-2 rounded-xl"><span class="text-zinc-400 text-[9px] block">Ruka</span><strong class="text-white">${w.arm || '—'} cm</strong></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-[10.5px]">
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 font-bold">Energia ${w.energy}/10</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 font-bold">Hlad ${w.hunger}/10</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 font-bold">Spánok ${w.sleep}/10</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300 font-bold">Regenerácia ${w.recovery}/10</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-300 font-bold">Plán ${w.adherence}/10</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-orange-500/15 text-orange-300 font-bold">Stres ${w.stress}/10</span>
                    </div>
                    ${w.note ? `<div class="text-xs text-zinc-300 bg-dark-850 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800/80">📝 ${w.note}</div>` : ''}
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DOPLNKY VÝŽIVY + OPRAVENÉ ZOSKUPENÉ PRIPOMIENKY DO KALENDÁRA (.ICS)
        // =====================================================================
        function getActiveProfileSuppStack() {
            if (!Array.isArray(userSupplementStacks[activeProfileId])) userSupplementStacks[activeProfileId] = [];
            return userSupplementStacks[activeProfileId];
        }
        function getTodayTakenSuppsSet() {
            const key = `${activeProfileId}_${getTodayISO()}`;
            if (!Array.isArray(suppTakenByDate[key])) suppTakenByDate[key] = [];
            return new Set(suppTakenByDate[key]);
        }
        function toggleSuppTakenToday(suppId) {
            const key = `${activeProfileId}_${getTodayISO()}`;
            if (!Array.isArray(suppTakenByDate[key])) suppTakenByDate[key] = [];
            const idx = suppTakenByDate[key].indexOf(suppId);
            if (idx === -1) suppTakenByDate[key].push(suppId);
            else suppTakenByDate[key].splice(idx, 1);
            saveState(); renderMySupplementStack();
        }
        function updateSuppBadgeCount() {
            const stack = getActiveProfileSuppStack();
            const badge = document.getElementById('domain-supp-badge'), countEl = document.getElementById('mystack-count');
            if (badge) badge.textContent = stack.length;
            if (countEl) countEl.textContent = stack.length;
        }
        function setSuppSubTab(subTab) {
            activeSuppSubTab = subTab;
            const myTab = document.getElementById('supp-tab-mystack'), catTab = document.getElementById('supp-tab-catalog');
            const myView = document.getElementById('supp-view-mystack'), catView = document.getElementById('supp-view-catalog');
            if (subTab === 'mystack') {
                myTab.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-zinc-800 text-amber-400 border border-amber-400/30 transition';
                catTab.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                myView.classList.remove('hidden'); catView.classList.add('hidden');
                renderMySupplementStack();
            } else {
                catTab.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold bg-zinc-800 text-amber-400 border border-amber-400/30 transition';
                myTab.className = 'py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition';
                catView.classList.remove('hidden'); myView.classList.add('hidden');
                renderSuppCategoryPills(); renderSupplementsCatalog();
            }
        }
        function renderSupplementsSection() { updateSuppBadgeCount(); setSuppSubTab(activeSuppSubTab); }
        function toggleSupplementInStack(suppId) {
            const stack = getActiveProfileSuppStack();
            const idx = stack.indexOf(suppId);
            const supp = supplements.find(s => s.id === suppId);
            if (idx === -1) { stack.push(suppId); if (supp) showToast(`Pridané: ${supp.name.split('(')[0].trim()}`); }
            else { stack.splice(idx, 1); if (supp) showToast(`Odobrané: ${supp.name.split('(')[0].trim()}`); }
            saveState(); updateSuppBadgeCount();
            if (activeSuppSubTab === 'mystack') renderMySupplementStack();
            else renderSupplementsCatalog();
        }
        function toggleSuppSlotAccordion(slotKey) { expandedSuppSlots[slotKey] = !expandedSuppSlots[slotKey]; renderMySupplementStack(); }
        function applyStarterSuppPack(packKey) {
            const packs = {
                health: ['supp-vit-d3k2', 'supp-vit-c', 'supp-omega3', 'supp-magnesium-bisglycinate'],
                strength: ['supp-creatine', 'supp-whey', 'supp-citrulline', 'supp-magnesium-bisglycinate'],
                runner: ['supp-joints', 'supp-electrolytes', 'supp-cordyceps', 'supp-magnesium-malate']
            };
            const stack = getActiveProfileSuppStack();
            (packs[packKey] || []).forEach(id => { if (!stack.includes(id)) stack.push(id); });
            saveState(); updateSuppBadgeCount(); renderMySupplementStack(); showToast('Balíček doplnkov pridaný do tvojho Stacku ✓');
        }
        function renderMySupplementStack() {
            const selectedSupps = getActiveProfileSuppStack().map(id => supplements.find(s => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
            const actionsBar = document.getElementById('mystack-actions-bar');
            const container = document.getElementById('mystack-timeline-container');
            if (!container) return;
            if (selectedSupps.length === 0) {
                actionsBar.classList.add('hidden');
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-8 px-4 bg-dark-900/60 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl"><h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-white mb-1">Zatiaľ nemáš zvolené doplnky</h3><p class="text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-4">Vyber si balíček vyššie alebo otvor Encyklopédiu doplnkov.</p><button onclick="setSuppSubTab('catalog')" class="bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl">Otvoriť Encyklopédiu</button></div>`;
                return;
            }
            const takenSet = getTodayTakenSuppsSet();
            actionsBar.classList.remove('hidden');
            container.innerHTML = ['morning_empty', 'with_meal', 'pre_workout', 'post_workout', 'evening'].map(slotKey => {
                const items = selectedSupps.filter(s => s.timingSlot === slotKey);
                if (items.length === 0) return '';
                const slotMeta = TIMING_SLOTS[slotKey];
                const isOpen = !!expandedSuppSlots[slotKey];
                const takenInSlot = items.filter(i => takenSet.has(i.id)).length;
                return `
                    <div class="bg-dark-900 border ${isOpen ? 'border-amber-400/50' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
                        <button type="button" onclick="toggleSuppSlotAccordion('${slotKey}')" class="w-full p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 text-left hover:bg-dark-850 transition">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                                <span class="text-lg shrink-0">${slotMeta.icon}</span>
                                <div class="min-w-0">
                                    <h3 class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-white">${slotMeta.title}</h3>
                                    <p class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 truncate mt-0.5">${isOpen ? slotMeta.subtitle : items.map(i => i.name.split('(')[0].trim()).join(' • ')}</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${slotMeta.color} shrink-0">${takenInSlot}/${items.length} ✓</span>
                        </button>
                        ${isOpen ? `<div class="px-3.5 pb-3.5 pt-1 border-t border-zinc-800/80 space-y-2.5">${items.map(s => {
                            const isTaken = takenSet.has(s.id);
                            return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border ${isTaken ? 'border-brand-500/45 bg-brand-500/5' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-xl p-3 space-y-1.5"><div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2"><div class="font-extrabold text-xs text-white">${s.name}</div><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"><button onclick="toggleSuppTakenToday('${s.id}')" class="text-[10px] font-extrabold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border ${isTaken ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 border-brand-400' : 'bg-dark-900 text-zinc-300 border-zinc-700'}">${isTaken ? '✓ Užité dnes' : 'Užiť'}</button><button onclick="toggleSupplementInStack('${s.id}')" class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30">✕</button></div></div><div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-400">💊 Dávka: ${s.dosage}</div><div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed"><span class="text-brand-400 font-bold">🔗 S čím kombinovať:</span> ${s.withWhat}</div><div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 leading-relaxed pt-1 border-t border-zinc-800/70">${s.benefit}</div></div>`;
                        }).join('')}</div>` : ''}
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }
        function renderSuppCategoryPills() {
            const container = document.getElementById('supp-category-pills');
            if (!container) return;
            container.innerHTML = SUPP_CATEGORIES.map(cat => `<button onclick="selectedSuppCategory='${cat.id}'; renderSuppCategoryPills(); renderSupplementsCatalog();" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition ${selectedSuppCategory === cat.id ? 'bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-900 text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-800 hover:text-white'}">${cat.name}</button>`).join('');
        }
        function renderSupplementsCatalog() {
            const container = document.getElementById('supp-catalog-list');
            if (!container) return;
            const query = (document.getElementById('search-supplement')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const stackIds = new Set(getActiveProfileSuppStack());
            const filtered = supplements.filter(s => (selectedSuppCategory === 'all' ? true : (selectedSuppCategory === 'with_meal_group' ? s.timingSlot === 'with_meal' : s.category === selectedSuppCategory)) && (!query || s.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || s.benefit.toLowerCase().includes(query)));
            container.innerHTML = filtered.map(s => {
                const inStack = stackIds.has(s.id);
                const slotMeta = TIMING_SLOTS[s.timingSlot] || TIMING_SLOTS.with_meal;
                return `<div class="bg-dark-900 border ${inStack ?
