'border-amber-400/50' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-2xl p-3.5 space-y-2.5"><div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${slotMeta.color}">${slotMeta.icon} ${slotMeta.badge}</span><h3 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white mt-1.5">${s.name}</h3></div><button onclick="toggleSupplementInStack('${s.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold shrink-0 ${inStack ? 'bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-800 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700'}">${inStack ? '✓ Užívam' : '+ Pridať'}</button></div><p class="text-xs text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">${s.benefit}</p><div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800/90 rounded-xl p-2.5 space-y-1 text-[11px]"><div><span class="text-amber-400 font-bold">⏰ Kedy:</span> <span class="text-zinc-200">${s.whenText}</span></div><div><span class="text-brand-400 font-bold">🔗 S čím kombinovať:</span> <span class="text-zinc-300">${s.withWhat}</span></div><div><span class="text-sky-400 font-bold">💊 Dávkovanie:</span> <span class="text-zinc-200 font-semibold">${s.dosage}</span></div></div></div>`;
            }).join('');
        }
        function openNewSupplementModal() { document.getElementById('modal-new-supplement').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        function closeNewSupplementModal() { document.getElementById('modal-new-supplement').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function createNewSupplement() {
            const name = document.getElementById('new-supp-name').value.trim();
            if (!name) return;
            const category = document.getElementById('new-supp-category').value;
            const timingSlot = document.getElementById('new-supp-timing-slot').value;
            const dosage = document.getElementById('new-supp-dosage').value.trim() || 'Podľa obalu';
            const desc = document.getElementById('new-supp-desc').value.trim() || 'Vlastný doplnok.';
            const slotMeta = TIMING_SLOTS[timingSlot] || TIMING_SLOTS.with_meal;
            const newSupp = { id: 'supp-custom-' + Date.now(), name, category, timingSlot, whenText: slotMeta.title, withWhat: 'S vodou.', dosage, benefit: desc };
            supplements.unshift(newSupp);
            getActiveProfileSuppStack().push(newSupp.id);
            saveState(); closeNewSupplementModal(); renderSupplementsSection(); showToast(`Pridané: ${name}`);
        }
        function copySupplementStackToClipboard() {
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            const selected = getActiveProfileSuppStack().map(id => supplements.find(s => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
            let text = `MM LAB – Supplement Stack (${prof.name})\n=====================================\n`;
            selected.forEach((s, i) => { text += `${i + 1}. ${s.name} (${s.dosage}) – ${s.whenText}\n`; });
            copyTextToClipboard(text, 'Zoznam doplnkov skopírovaný!');
        }

        // OPRAVENÝ EXPORT DOPLNKOV DO KALENDÁRA (1 ZOSKUPENÁ UDALOSŤ NA ČASOVÝ BLOK, ČISTÝ NÁZOV BEZ ČÍSEL)
        function exportSupplementsToICS() {
            const selected = getActiveProfileSuppStack().map(id => supplements.find(s => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
            if (selected.length === 0) return;
            const slotHours = {
                morning_empty: '080000',
                with_meal: '123000',
                pre_workout: '163000',
                post_workout: '183000',
                evening: '213000'
            };
            const todayCompact = getTodayISO().replace(/-/g, '');
            const dtStamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, '').split('.')[0] + 'Z';
            const lines = [
                'BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
                'VERSION:2.0',
                'PRODID:-//MM LAB//Supplements//SK',
                'CALSCALE:GREGORIAN'
            ];

            Object.keys(slotHours).forEach(slotKey => {
                const items = selected.filter(s => s.timingSlot === slotKey);
                if (items.length === 0) return;
                const meta = TIMING_SLOTS[slotKey];
                const shortNames = items.map(i => i.name.split('(')[0].trim()).join(' + ');
                const summary = `${meta.icon} MM LAB ${meta.badge}: ${shortNames}`.replace(/,/g, '\\,');
                const descLines = [
                    `${meta.title} (${meta.subtitle})`,
                    ...items.map(i => `• ${i.name.split('(')[0].trim()}: ${i.dosage}`)
                ].join('\\n').replace(/,/g, '\\,');

                lines.push(
                    'BEGIN:VEVENT',
                    `UID:mmlab-supp-${slotKey}-${Date.now()}@miromedofit.lab`,
                    `DTSTAMP:${dtStamp}`,
                    `DTSTART:${todayCompact}T${slotHours[slotKey]}`,
                    'DURATION:PT10M',
                    'RRULE:FREQ=DAILY',
                    `SUMMARY:${summary}`,
                    `DESCRIPTION:${descLines}`,
                    'BEGIN:VALARM',
                    'TRIGGER:-PT0M',
                    'ACTION:DISPLAY',
                    `DESCRIPTION:${summary}`,
                    'END:VALARM',
                    'END:VEVENT'
                );
            });

            lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');
            const icsContent = lines.join('\r\n');
            const blob = new Blob([icsContent], { type: 'text/calendar;charset=utf-8' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = 'MM_LAB_Vitaminy.ics'; a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url); showToast('Pripomienky vitamínov (.ics) stiahnuté ✓');
        }

        // PROFILY, TRÉNINGY, SPLITY, DATABÁZA & HISTÓRIA
        // =====================================================================
        function openProfileModal() {
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            document.getElementById('editing-profile-current-label').textContent = prof.name;
            document.getElementById('rename-active-profile-input').value = prof.name;
            applyAdminPermissionsUI(); renderProfilesList();
            document.getElementById('modal-profiles').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function closeProfileModal() { document.getElementById('modal-profiles').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function renameActiveProfile() {
            const newName = document.getElementById('rename-active-profile-input').value.trim();
            if (!newName) return;
            getActiveProfile().name = newName;
            saveState(); updateHeaderProfileUI();
            document.getElementById('editing-profile-current-label').textContent = newName;
            renderProfilesList(); showToast(`Meno uložené: ${newName}`);
        }
        function renderProfilesList() {
            const visibleProfiles = isTrainerOrAdmin() ? profiles : profiles.filter(p => p.id === 'me');
            document.getElementById('profiles-list').innerHTML = visibleProfiles.map(p => {
                const isSelected = p.id === activeProfileId, isPrimary = p.id === 'me';
                const count = workoutHistory.filter(w => (w.profileId || 'me') === p.id).length;
                return `<div onclick="selectProfile('${p.id}')" class="cursor-pointer p-3 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-between gap-2 ${isSelected ? 'bg-brand-500/15 border-brand-500/50' : 'bg-dark-850 border-zinc-800'}"><div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold ${isPrimary ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950'}">${p.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-white truncate">${p.name}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">${count} tréningov</div></div></div>${(!isPrimary && isTrainerOrAdmin()) ? `<button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteClientProfile('${p.id}')" class="p-1 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400">✕</button>` : ''}</div>`;
            }).join('');
        }
        function selectProfile(profileId) {
            if (!isTrainerOrAdmin() && profileId !== 'me') return;
            activeProfileId = profileId; saveState(); updateHeaderProfileUI(); initBiometricsUI();
            renderProfilesList(); renderDatabase(); renderActiveWorkout(); renderHistory(); renderNutritionSection(); renderSupplementsSection();
            showToast(`Prepnuté na: ${getActiveProfile().name}`);
        }
        function addNewClientProfile() {
            if (!isTrainerOrAdmin()) { showToast('Pridávanie klientov je povolené len trénerovi'); return; }
            const input = document.getElementById('new-client-name');
            const name = input.value.trim();
            if (!name) return;
            const newProf = { id: 'client-' + Date.now(), name, role: 'client' };
            profiles.push(newProf); input.value = ''; selectProfile(newProf.id); closeProfileModal();
        }
        function deleteClientProfile(profileId) {
            if (profileId === 'me') return;
            profiles = profiles.filter(p => p.id !== profileId);
            if (activeProfileId === profileId) activeProfileId = 'me';
            saveState(); updateHeaderProfileUI(); renderProfilesList();
        }

        function saveWorkoutMeta() { localStorage.setItem('fitlog_workout_title', document.getElementById('workout-title').value); }
        function startWorkoutTimer() {
            if (workoutTimerInterval) clearInterval(workoutTimerInterval);
            updateTimerDisplay();
            workoutTimerInterval = setInterval(updateTimerDisplay, 1000);
        }
        function updateTimerDisplay() {
            const el = document.getElementById('workout-timer-display');
            if (!el) return;
            if (activeWorkout.length === 0 || !workoutStartTime) { el.textContent = '00:00'; return; }
            const sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - workoutStartTime) / 1000));
            const m = Math.floor(sec / 60), s = sec % 60;
            el.textContent = `${m < 10 ? '0' : ''}${m}:${s < 10 ? '0' : ''}${s}`;
        }
        function calculateTotalVolume(list) {
            let total = 0;
            (list || []).forEach(ex => {
                const done = (ex.sets || []).filter(s => s.completed);
                const toSum = done.length > 0 ? done : (ex.sets || []);
                toSum.forEach(s => {
                    const w = parseFloat(s.weight) || 0;
                    if (w <= 0) return;
                    total += s.mode === 'time' ? w : w * (parseInt(s.reps) || 0);
                });
            });
            return Math.round(total * 10) / 10;
        }
        function formatVolumeKg(kg) { return `${kg.toLocaleString('sk-SK')} kg`; }
        function formatDuration(totalSeconds) {
            const sec = parseInt(totalSeconds) || 0;
            if (sec < 60) return `${sec} s`;
            const m = Math.floor(sec / 60), s = sec % 60;
            return s === 0 ? `${m} min` : `${m}:${s < 10 ? '0' : ''}${s} min`;
        }
        function getRirBadgeStyle(rir) {
            const val = parseInt(rir);
            if (val === 0) return 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/40';
            if (val <= 2) return 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/40';
            if (val <= 4) return 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/40';
            return 'bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400 border-sky-500/40';
        }
        function getRirDescription(rir) {
            const val = parseInt(rir);
            if (val === 0) return 'Do úplného zlyhania (0 v rezerve)';
            if (val === 1) return '1 opakovanie v rezerve (veľmi ťažké)';
            if (val === 2) return '2 opakovania v rezerve (ideálne pracovné)';
            if (val === 3) return '3 opakovania v rezerve (kontrolované)';
            return `${val} opakovaní v rezerve`;
        }
        function getTechniqueMeta(techId) { return SET_TECHNIQUES.find(t => t.id === techId) || SET_TECHNIQUES[0]; }

        function getExerciseIllustrationSVG(exerciseName, category) {
            const name = (exerciseName || '').toLowerCase();
            if (name.includes('plank')) return `<svg viewBox="0 0 120 76" class="w-full h-full"><line x1="12" y1="62" x2="108" y2="62" stroke="#2e2e36" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="92" cy="42" r="5" fill="#10b981"/><line x1="88" y1="45" x2="42" y2="49" stroke="#f4f4f5" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"/><line x1="42" y1="49" x2="22" y2="60" stroke="#f4f4f5" stroke-width="3.5" stroke-linecap="round"/><polyline points="85,46 85,60 95,60" fill="none" stroke="#34d399" stroke-width="3"/></svg>`;
            if (category === 'plyo' || name.includes('pogo') || name.includes('skok')) return `<svg viewBox="0 0 120 76" class="w-full h-full"><line x1="18" y1="66" x2="102" y2="66" stroke="#2e2e36" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="60" cy="14" r="5" fill="#10b981"/><line x1="60" y1="20" x2="60" y2="36" stroke="#f4f4f5" stroke-width="4"/><polyline points="60,36 53,45 55,54" fill="none" stroke="#f4f4f5" stroke-width="3.5"/><polyline points="60,36 67,45 65,54" fill="none" stroke="#f4f4f5" stroke-width="3.5"/></svg>`;
            return `<svg viewBox="0 0 120 76" class="w-full h-full"><line x1="26" y1="38" x2="94" y2="38" stroke="#a1a1aa" stroke-width="3.5"/><rect x="22" y="22" width="6" height="32" rx="2" fill="#10b981"/><rect x="92" y="22" width="6" height="32" rx="2" fill="#10b981"/></svg>`;
        }

        function getLastPerformance(exerciseId) {
            for (let i = 0; i < workoutHistory.length; i++) {
                const w = workoutHistory[i];
                if ((w.profileId || 'me') !== activeProfileId) continue;
                const found = (w.exercises || []).find(e => e.exerciseId === exerciseId);
                if (found && found.sets?.length > 0) {
                    const valid = found.sets.filter(s => s.completed);
                    const setsToUse = valid.length > 0 ? valid : found.sets;
                    let maxW = 0, maxDur = 0;
                    setsToUse.forEach(s => {
                        const wn = parseFloat(s.weight) || 0;
                        if (wn > maxW) maxW = wn;
                        if (s.mode === 'time') maxDur = Math.max(maxDur, parseInt(s.duration) || 0);
                    });
                    const headline = maxW > 0 ? `${maxW} kg` : (maxDur > 0 ? formatDuration(maxDur) : `${setsToUse[0].reps} opak.`);
                    const setsText = setsToUse.map(s => `${s.weight ? s.weight + 'kg' : 'BW'}×${s.mode === 'time' ? formatDuration(s.duration) : s.reps + 'x'} (RIR ${s.rir})`).join(', ');
                    return { date: w.date, headline, setsText, sets: setsToUse, maxWeight: maxW };
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        function toggleBeginnerSplitGuide() {
            const body = document.getElementById('beginner-split-guide-body'), arrow = document.getElementById('split-guide-arrow');
            const isHidden = body.classList.contains('hidden');
            body.classList.toggle('hidden');
            arrow.textContent = isHidden ? '▲' : '▼';
        }

        function renderSplits() {
            const container = document.getElementById('splits-list');
            if (!container) return;
            const coach = isTrainerOrAdmin();
            container.innerHTML = savedSplits.map(split => {
                const blocksHtml = split.blocks.map((block, idx) => {
                    const ex1 = exercises.find(e => e.id === block.exerciseId);
                    const tech = getTechniqueMeta(block.technique);
                    if (block.type === 'superset') {
                        const ex2 = exercises.find(e => e.id === block.exerciseId2);
                        return `<div class="bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/30 rounded-xl p-2.5 space-y-1"><span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-purple-300 bg-purple-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">SUPERSÉRIA • ${block.sets}×</span><div class="text-xs font-bold text-white pl-1">A1: ${ex1 ? ex1.name : ''}</div><div class="text-xs font-bold text-purple-200 pl-1">A2: ${ex2 ? ex2.name : ''}</div></div>`;
                    }
                    return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-200 truncate">${idx + 1}. ${ex1 ? ex1.name : ''}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">${block.sets} série</div></div>${tech.badge ? `<span class="text-[9px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${tech.style}">${tech.badge}</span>` : ''}</div>`;
                }).join('');
                return `<div class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3"><div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2"><div><span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-brand-400 bg-brand-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">${split.tag}</span><h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-white mt-1.5">${split.name}</h3></div><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">${coach ? `<button onclick="openSendSplitModal('${split.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-400/15 hover:bg-amber-400/25 border border-amber-400/35 text-amber-300 text-[11px] font-extrabold">📤 Poslať ľuďom</button>` : ''}<button onclick="deleteSplit('${split.id}')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-dark-800 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400">✕</button></div></div><div class="space-y-1.5">${blocksHtml}</div><button onclick="startWorkoutFromSplit('${split.id}')" class="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-brand-500 hover:bg-brand-600 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs">▶ Spustiť tento tréning</button></div>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        let pendingSplitIdToSend = null, cachedSharedSplits = [];
        async function openSendSplitModal(splitId) {
            if (!isTrainerOrAdmin()) return;
            const split = savedSplits.find(s => s.id === splitId);
            if (!split) return;
            pendingSplitIdToSend = splitId;
            document.getElementById('send-split-title-label').textContent = split.name;
            document.getElementById('send-split-note-input').value = '';
            const sel = document.getElementById('send-split-target-select');
            sel.innerHTML = `<option value="ALL">👥 Všetkým mojim klientom</option>`;
            document.getElementById('modal-send-split').classList.remove('hidden');
            try {
                const testers = await supabaseFetch('beta_testers?is_active=eq.true&select=code,name,trainer_code');
                const myCode = (currentTesterSession?.code || '').toUpperCase();
                (testers || []).forEach(t => {
                    if (t.code === myCode) return;
                    if (isSuperAdmin() || (t.trainer_code || '').toUpperCase() === myCode) {
                        const opt = document.createElement('option');
                        opt.value = t.code;
                        opt.textContent = `👤 ${t.name} (${t.code})`;
                        sel.appendChild(opt);
                    }
                });
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        async function confirmSendSplitToTesters() {
            if (!isTrainerOrAdmin() || !pendingSplitIdToSend) return;
            const split = savedSplits.find(s => s.id === pendingSplitIdToSend);
            if (!split) return;
            const targetCode = document.getElementById('send-split-target-select').value || 'ALL';
            const note = document.getElementById('send-split-note-input').value.trim();
            const btn = document.getElementById('send-split-confirm-btn');
            btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Odosielam...';
            try {
                await supabaseFetch('tester_logs', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Prefer': 'return=minimal' }, body: JSON.stringify({ tester_code: currentTesterSession.code, log_type: 'shared_split', payload: { targetCode, trainerCode: currentTesterSession.code, note, split: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(split)) } }) });
                document.getElementById('modal-send-split').classList.add('hidden');
                showToast(targetCode === 'ALL' ? 'Split odoslaný klientom ✓' : `Split odoslaný pre ${targetCode} ✓`);
            } catch (e) { showToast('Chyba odosielania – skontroluj internet'); }
            finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Odoslať plán ✓'; }
        }
        async function fetchSharedTrainerSplits(notifyIfEmpty = false) {
            if (!currentTesterSession?.code) return;
            const box = document.getElementById('shared-trainer-splits-box'), listEl = document.getElementById('shared-trainer-splits-list');
            if (!box || !listEl) return;
            try {
                const rows = await supabaseFetch('tester_logs?log_type=eq.shared_split&select=id,payload,created_at&order=created_at.desc&limit=15');
                const myCode = currentTesterSession.code.toUpperCase();
                const myTrainer = (currentTesterSession.trainer_code || '').toUpperCase();
                const matching = (rows || []).filter(r => {
                    const t = (r.payload?.targetCode || 'ALL').toUpperCase();
                    const sender = (r.payload?.trainerCode || 'MIRO-ADMIN').toUpperCase();
                    if (t === myCode) return true;
                    if (t === 'ALL' && !isSoloUser() && sender === myTrainer) return true;
                    return false;
                });
                cachedSharedSplits = matching;
                if (matching.length === 0) { box.classList.add('hidden'); if (notifyIfEmpty) showToast('Zatiaľ žiadne nové plány od trénera'); return; }
                box.classList.remove('hidden');
                listEl.innerHTML = matching.map((item, idx) => {
                    const sp = item.payload?.split;
                    if (!sp) return '';
                    const note = item.payload?.note || '';
                    return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-white truncate">${sp.name}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 truncate">${sp.blocks?.length || 0} cvikov ${note ? `• 📝 ${note}` : ''}</div></div><button type="button" onclick="importSharedTrainerSplit(${idx})" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-300 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-[11px] shrink-0">⬇ Pridať</button></div>`;
                }).join('');
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        function importSharedTrainerSplit(idx) {
            const sp = cachedSharedSplits[idx]?.payload?.split;
            if (!sp) return;
            const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sp));
            copy.id = 'split-trainer-' + Date.now();
            savedSplits.unshift(copy);
            saveState(); renderSplits(); showToast(`Plán "${copy.name}" pridaný do tvojich splitov ✓`);
        }

        function startWorkoutFromSplit(splitId) {
            const split = savedSplits.find(s => s.id === splitId);
            if (!split) return;
            editingHistoryId = null;
            const newWorkoutExercises = [];
            let ssCounter = 1;
            split.blocks.forEach(block => {
                if (block.type === 'superset') {
                    const ssGroup = `SS-${ssCounter++}`;
                    [block.exerciseId, block.exerciseId2].forEach((exId, subIdx) => {
                        const exObj = exercises.find(e => e.id === exId);
                        if (!exObj) return;
                        const lastPerf = getLastPerformance(exObj.id);
                        const sets = Array.from({ length: parseInt(block.sets) || 3 }, (_, i) => {
                            const prev = lastPerf?.sets[i];
                            return { mode: prev ? prev.mode : exObj.defaultMode, weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: prev ? prev.weight : '', prevReps: prev ? prev.reps : '', duration: prev ? prev.duration : 45, rir: prev ? prev.rir : 2, technique: 'superset', completed: false };
                        });
                        newWorkoutExercises.push({ exerciseId: exObj.id, name: exObj.name, category: exObj.category, supersetGroup: ssGroup, supersetLabel: subIdx === 0 ? 'A1' : 'A2', sets });
                    });
                } else {
                    const exObj = exercises.find(e => e.id === block.exerciseId);
                    if (!exObj) return;
                    const lastPerf = getLastPerformance(exObj.id);
                    const sets = Array.from({ length: parseInt(block.sets) || 3 }, (_, i) => {
                        const prev = lastPerf?.sets[i];
                        return { mode: prev ? prev.mode : exObj.defaultMode, weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: prev ? prev.weight : '', prevReps: prev ? prev.reps : '', duration: prev ? prev.duration : 45, rir: prev ? prev.rir : 2, technique: block.technique || 'standard', completed: false };
                    });
                    newWorkoutExercises.push({ exerciseId: exObj.id, name: exObj.name, category: exObj.category, sets });
                }
            });
            activeWorkout = newWorkoutExercises; expandedExerciseIndex = 0; workoutStartTime = Date.now();
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_workout_start', workoutStartTime.toString());
            document.getElementById('workout-title').value = split.name;
            saveWorkoutMeta(); saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); switchTab('workout'); showToast(`Spustený plán: ${split.name}`);
        }
        function deleteSplit(splitId) { savedSplits = savedSplits.filter(s => s.id !== splitId); saveState(); renderSplits(); }

        function openSplitBuilderModal() {
            draftSplitBlocks = [];
            document.getElementById('split-builder-name').value = '';
            const catOptions = CATEGORIES.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.id === 'all' ? 'Všetky partie' : c.name}</option>`).join('');
            const cat1 = document.getElementById('builder-cat1-select'), cat2 = document.getElementById('builder-cat2-select');
            if (cat1) cat1.innerHTML = catOptions;
            if (cat2) cat2.innerHTML = catOptions;
            populateBuilderExerciseSelect(1); populateBuilderExerciseSelect(2);
            setSplitBuilderMode('single'); renderDraftSplitBlocks();
            document.getElementById('modal-split-builder').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function populateBuilderExerciseSelect(slotNum) {
            const catSel = document.getElementById(`builder-cat${slotNum}-select`), exSel = document.getElementById(`builder-ex${slotNum}-select`);
            if (!catSel || !exSel) return;
            const catId = catSel.value || 'all';
            exSel.innerHTML = exercises.filter(e => catId === 'all' || e.category === catId).map(ex => `<option value="${ex.id}">${ex.name}</option>`).join('');
        }
        function closeSplitBuilderModal() { document.getElementById('modal-split-builder').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function setSplitBuilderMode(mode) {
            builderMode = mode;
            document.getElementById('builder-ex2-wrapper').classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== 'superset');
            document.getElementById('builder-mode-superset').className = mode === 'superset' ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold bg-purple-600 text-white' : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400';
            document.getElementById('builder-mode-single').className = mode === 'single' ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold bg-zinc-700 text-white' : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400';
        }
        function addBlockToDraftSplit() {
            const ex1 = document.getElementById('builder-ex1-select').value, ex2 = document.getElementById('builder-ex2-select').value;
            const sets = parseInt(document.getElementById('builder-sets-count').value) || 3, technique = document.getElementById('builder-technique-select').value;
            draftSplitBlocks.push(builderMode === 'superset' ? { type: 'superset', exerciseId: ex1, exerciseId2: ex2, sets, technique: 'superset' } : { type: 'single', exerciseId: ex1, sets, technique });
            renderDraftSplitBlocks();
        }
        function renderDraftSplitBlocks() {
            document.getElementById('draft-blocks-count').textContent = draftSplitBlocks.length;
            document.getElementById('draft-split-blocks-list').innerHTML = draftSplitBlocks.map((b, i) => {
                const e1 = exercises.find(x => x.id === b.exerciseId);
                return `<div class="bg-dark-850 border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 flex justify-between text-xs"><span class="font-bold text-white">${i + 1}. ${e1?.name || ''} (${b.sets}×)</span><button type="button" onclick="draftSplitBlocks.splice(${i},1);renderDraftSplitBlocks();" class="text-rose-400">✕</button></div>`;
            }).join('');
        }
        function saveNewSplit() {
            const name = document.getElementById('split-builder-name').value.trim();
            if (!name || draftSplitBlocks.length === 0) return;
            savedSplits.unshift({ id: 'split-' + Date.now(), name, tag: document.getElementById('split-builder-tag').value, blocks: [...draftSplitBlocks] });
            saveState(); renderSplits(); closeSplitBuilderModal();
        }

        function renderCategoryPills() {
            document.getElementById('category-pills').innerHTML = CATEGORIES.map(cat => `<button onclick="selectedCategory='${cat.id}'; renderCategoryPills(); renderDatabase();" class="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition ${selectedCategory === cat.id ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-900 text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-800 hover:text-white'}">${cat.name}</button>`).join('');
        }
        function renderDatabase() {
            const container = document.getElementById('database-list');
            const query = (document.getElementById('search-exercise').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const cats = CATEGORIES.filter(c => c.id !== 'all' && (selectedCategory === 'all' || selectedCategory === c.id));
            container.innerHTML = cats.map(cat => {
                const list = exercises.filter(ex => ex.category === cat.id && (!query || ex.name.toLowerCase().includes(query)));
                if (list.length === 0) return '';
                return `<div class="space-y-2"><div class="flex items-center justify-between px-1"><h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-brand-400">${cat.name}</h3><span class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">${list.length} cvikov</span></div><div class="space-y-2">${list.map(ex => {
                    const lastPerf = getLastPerformance(ex.id);
                    return `<div class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-800/90 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5"><div onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${ex.id}')" class="cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="w-11 h-8 rounded-xl bg-dark-950 border border-zinc-800 p-1 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">${getExerciseIllustrationSVG(ex.name, ex.category)}</div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><h4 class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white leading-snug">${ex.name}</h4><div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5 flex-wrap">${lastPerf ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded">Naposledy: ${lastPerf.headline}</span>` : `<span class="text-[10px] text-sky-400">📘 Technika & Graf</span>`}</div></div></div><button onclick="addExerciseToWorkout('${ex.id}')" class="bg-brand-500/15 hover:bg-brand-500 text-brand-400 hover:text-zinc-950 border border-brand-500/30 font-extrabold text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-xl shrink-0">+ Pridať</button></div>`;
                }).join('')}</div></div>`;
            }).join('');
        }
        function openNewExerciseModal() { document.getElementById('modal-new-exercise').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        function closeNewExerciseModal() { document.getElementById('modal-new-exercise').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function createNewExercise() {
            const name = document.getElementById('new-ex-name').value.trim();
            if (!name) return;
            exercises.unshift({ id: 'ex-custom-' + Date.now(), name, category: document.getElementById('new-ex-category').value, defaultMode: document.querySelector('input[name="new-ex-type"]:checked').value });
            saveState(); document.getElementById('new-ex-name').value = ''; closeNewExerciseModal(); renderDatabase(); showToast(`Cvik "${name}" pridaný!`);
        }

        function addExerciseToWorkout(exerciseId) {
            const exObj = exercises.find(e => e.id === exerciseId);
            if (!exObj) return;
            if (activeWorkout.length === 0) { workoutStartTime = Date.now(); localStorage.setItem('fitlog_workout_start', workoutStartTime.toString()); }
            const lastPerf = getLastPerformance(exObj.id);
            const initialSets = lastPerf?.sets?.length > 0
                ? lastPerf.sets.map(s => ({ mode: s.mode || exObj.defaultMode, weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: s.weight || '', prevReps: s.reps || '', duration: s.duration || 45, rir: s.rir ?? 2, technique: s.technique || 'standard', completed: false }))
                : [{ mode: exObj.defaultMode, weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: '', prevReps: '', duration: 45, rir: 2, technique: 'standard', completed: false }];
            activeWorkout.push({ exerciseId: exObj.id, name: exObj.name, category: exObj.category, sets: initialSets });
            expandedExerciseIndex = activeWorkout.length - 1;
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); switchTab('workout'); showToast(`Pridané: ${exObj.name}`);
        }
        function setExpandedExercise(idx) { expandedExerciseIndex = idx; saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); }
        function removeExerciseFromWorkout(exIdx) {
            const target = activeWorkout[exIdx];
            if (target?.supersetGroup) activeWorkout = activeWorkout.filter(item => item.supersetGroup !== target.supersetGroup);
            else activeWorkout.splice(exIdx, 1);
            if (expandedExerciseIndex >= activeWorkout.length) expandedExerciseIndex = Math.max(0, activeWorkout.length - 1);
            if (activeWorkout.length === 0) { workoutStartTime = null; localStorage.removeItem('fitlog_workout_start'); }
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
        }
        function addSet(exIdx) {
            const ex = activeWorkout[exIdx];
            if (!ex) return;
            const targets = ex.supersetGroup ? activeWorkout.filter(item => item.supersetGroup === ex.supersetGroup) : [ex];
            targets.forEach(item => {
                const last = item.sets[item.sets.length - 1];
                item.sets.push(last
                    ? { mode: last.mode, weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: last.weight || last.prevWeight || '', prevReps: last.reps || last.prevReps || '', duration: last.duration || 45, rir: last.rir ?? 2, technique: last.technique || 'standard', completed: false }
                    : { mode: 'reps', weight: '', reps: '', prevWeight: '', prevReps: '', duration: 45, rir: 2, technique: 'standard', completed: false });
            });
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
        }
        function updateWorkoutStatsHeader() {
            const exCountEl = document.getElementById('stat-exercises'), setCountEl = document.getElementById('stat-sets');
            const volEl = document.getElementById('stat-volume'), finishBtn = document.getElementById('header-finish-btn');
            let totalSets = 0;
            activeWorkout.forEach(ex => { totalSets += (ex.sets || []).length; });
            if (exCountEl) exCountEl.textContent = activeWorkout.length;
            if (setCountEl) setCountEl.textContent = totalSets;
            if (volEl) volEl.textContent = formatVolumeKg(calculateTotalVolume(activeWorkout));
            if (finishBtn) finishBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', activeWorkout.length === 0);
        }
        function updateSetField(exIdx, setIdx, field, value) {
            if (!activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx]) return;
            activeWorkout[exIdx].sets[setIdx][field] = value;
            saveState(); updateWorkoutStatsHeader();
        }
        function adjustSetNumber(exIdx, setIdx, field, delta, minVal = 0) {
            const set = activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx];
            if (!set) return;
            const fallback = field === 'weight' ? (parseFloat(set.prevWeight) || 0) : (parseInt(set.prevReps) || 0);
            const current = set[field] === '' || set[field] === undefined ? fallback : parseFloat(set[field]) || 0;
            set[field] = Math.max(minVal, Math.round((current + delta) * 10) / 10);
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
        }
        function toggleSetMode(exIdx, setIdx) {
            const set = activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx];
            if (!set) return;
            set.mode = set.mode === 'time' ? 'reps' : 'time';
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
        }
        function removeSet(exIdx, setIdx) {
            const ex = activeWorkout[exIdx];
            if (!ex || ex.sets.length <= 1) return;
            ex.sets.splice(setIdx, 1); saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
        }
        function toggleSetCompleted(exIdx, setIdx) {
            const set = activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx];
            if (!set) return;
            set.completed = !set.completed;
            saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
            if (set.completed) startRestTimer(90);
        }
        function openTimeModal(exIdx, setIdx) {
            const set = activeWorkout[exIdx]?.sets[setIdx];
            if (!set) return;
            editingTimeTarget = { exIndex: exIdx, setIndex: setIdx };
            const totalSec = parseInt(set.duration) || 30;
            document.getElementById('time-input-min').value = Math.floor(totalSec / 60);
            document.getElementById('time-input-sec').value = totalSec % 60;
            document.getElementById('modal-time-picker').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function closeTimeModal() { document.getElementById('modal-time-picker').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function applyPresetTime(seconds) {
            const { exIndex, setIndex } = editingTimeTarget;
            if (exIndex !== null && setIndex !== null && activeWorkout[exIndex]?.sets[setIndex]) {
                activeWorkout[exIndex].sets[setIndex].duration = seconds;
                saveState(); renderActiveWorkout();
            }
            closeTimeModal();
        }
        function saveCustomTime() {
            const mins = Math.max(0, parseInt(document.getElementById('time-input-min').value) || 0);
            const secs = Math.max(0, parseInt(document.getElementById('time-input-sec').value) || 0);
            applyPresetTime(Math.max(1, mins * 60 + secs));
        }

        function renderSingleSetCardHtml(ex, exIdx, set, setIdx, accentLabel = '') {
            return `
                <div class="bg-dark-850 border ${set.completed ? 'border-brand-500/50 bg-brand-500/5' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-2xl p-3 space-y-2.5">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                            <span class="px-2 h-6 rounded-lg bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-700 text-xs font-extrabold text-zinc-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">${accentLabel || (setIdx + 1)}</span>
                            <select onchange="updateSetField(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'technique', this.value); renderActiveWorkout();" class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-700/80 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-200 truncate">
                                ${SET_TECHNIQUES.map(t => `<option value="${t.id}" ${set.technique === t.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${t.label}</option>`).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                            <button onclick="toggleSetMode(${exIdx}, ${setIdx})" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-700 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-300">${set.mode === 'time' ? '⏱ Čas' : '🔢 Opak.'}</button>
                            ${ex.sets.length > 1 ? `<button onclick="removeSet(${exIdx}, ${setIdx})" class="p-1 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400">✕</button>` : ''}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 items-center">
                        <div class="col-span-5">
                            <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-zinc-400 mb-1">Váha (kg)</label>
                            <div class="flex items-center bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                                <button type="button" onclick="adjustSetNumber(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'weight', -2.5, 0)" class="px-2 py-2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-dark-800 font-black text-sm">−</button>
                                <input type="number" step="0.5" placeholder="${set.prevWeight || '0'}" value="${set.weight}" oninput="updateSetField(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'weight', this.value)" class="w-full min-w-0 bg-transparent py-2 text-center text-sm font-extrabold text-white placeholder-zinc-600 focus:outline-none">
                                <button type="button" onclick="adjustSetNumber(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'weight', 2.5, 0)" class="px-2 py-2 text-brand-400 hover:bg-dark-800 font-black text-sm">+</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-span-5">
                            ${set.mode === 'time' ? `
                                <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-sky-400 mb-1">Trvanie</label>
                                <button onclick="openTimeModal(${exIdx}, ${setIdx})" class="w-full bg-dark-900 border border-sky-500/40 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-center text-sm font-extrabold text-sky-300">${formatDuration(set.duration)}</button>
                            ` : `
                                <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-zinc-400 mb-1">Opakovania</label>
                                <div class="flex items-center bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                                    <button type="button" onclick="adjustSetNumber(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'reps', -1, 0)" class="px-2 py-2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-dark-800 font-black text-sm">−</button>
                                    <input type="number" placeholder="${set.prevReps || '0'}" value="${set.reps}" oninput="updateSetField(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'reps', this.value)" class="w-full min-w-0 bg-transparent py-2 text-center text-sm font-extrabold text-white placeholder-zinc-600 focus:outline-none">
                                    <button type="button" onclick="adjustSetNumber(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'reps', 1, 0)" class="px-2 py-2 text-brand-400 hover:bg-dark-800 font-black text-sm">+</button>
                                </div>
                            `}
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-span-2 pt-4">
                            <button onclick="toggleSetCompleted(${exIdx}, ${setIdx})" class="w-full h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-extrabold text-sm border ${set.completed ? 'bg-brand-500 border-brand-400 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-900 border-zinc-700 text-zinc-400'}">✓</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    ${set.mode === 'time' ? `<button onclick="startLiveSetCountdown(${exIdx}, ${setIdx})" class="w-full py-2 rounded-xl bg-sky-500/15 hover:bg-sky-500/25 border border-sky-500/40 text-sky-300 font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition"><span>▶ Spustiť živý odpočet (${formatDuration(set.duration)})</span></button>` : ''}
                    <div class="pt-1 border-t border-zinc-800/80">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-400">${getRirDescription(set.rir)}</span>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <button onclick="openEduModal('rir')" class="text-[10px] font-bold text-sky-400 underline">Čo je RIR?</button>
                                <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${getRirBadgeStyle(set.rir)}">RIR ${set.rir}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <input type="range" min="0" max="10" value="${set.rir}" oninput="updateSetField(${exIdx}, ${setIdx}, 'rir', this.value); renderActiveWorkout();" class="w-full accent-emerald-500 h-1.5 bg-dark-900 rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function renderActiveWorkout() {
            const container = document.getElementById('active-exercises-container');
            const emptyState = document.getElementById('empty-workout-state');
            updateWorkoutStatsHeader();
            if (activeWorkout.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = ''; emptyState.classList.remove('hidden'); return; }
            emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
            if (expandedExerciseIndex >= activeWorkout.length || expandedExerciseIndex < 0) expandedExerciseIndex = 0;

            const blocks = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < activeWorkout.length; i++) {
                const cur = activeWorkout[i], next = activeWorkout[i + 1];
                if (cur.supersetGroup && next && next.supersetGroup === cur.supersetGroup) {
                    blocks.push({ type: 'superset', items: [{ ex: cur, exIdx: i }, { ex: next, exIdx: i + 1 }] });
                    i++;
                } else {
                    blocks.push({ type: 'single', items: [{ ex: cur, exIdx: i }] });
                }
            }

            container.innerHTML = blocks.map((block, bIdx) => {
                const isExpanded = block.items.some(it => it.exIdx === expandedExerciseIndex);
                const firstIdx = block.items[0].exIdx;
                const lastBlockIdx = block.items[block.items.length - 1].exIdx;
                const isLastBlock = lastBlockIdx === activeWorkout.length - 1;
                const nextBlockFirstIdx = !isLastBlock ? blocks[bIdx + 1].items[0].exIdx : null;

                if (block.type === 'superset') {
                    const [a1, a2] = block.items;
                    const allDone = block.items.every(it => it.ex.sets.every(s => s.completed) && it.ex.sets.length > 0);
                    const totalSetsCount = Math.max(a1.ex.sets.length, a2.ex.sets.length);
                    const donePairs = Math.min(a1.ex.sets.filter(s => s.completed).length, a2.ex.sets.filter(s => s.completed).length);

                    if (!isExpanded) {
                        return `<div onclick="setExpandedExercise(${firstIdx})" class="cursor-pointer bg-dark-900 hover:bg-dark-850 border ${allDone ? 'border-brand-500/40' : 'border-purple-500/35'} rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0"><div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-black shrink-0 ${allDone ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40'}">${allDone ? '✓' : 'SS'}</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-purple-400">SUPERSÉRIA (${donePairs}/${totalSetsCount} kôl)</div><h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">A1: ${a1.ex.name} + A2: ${a2.ex.name}</h4></div></div><span class="text-xs font-bold text-purple-300 shrink-0">Otvoriť →</span></div>`;
                    }

                    const lastPerf1 = getLastPerformance(a1.ex.exerciseId), lastPerf2 = getLastPerformance(a2.ex.exerciseId);
                    const roundsHtml = Array.from({ length: totalSetsCount }, (_, sIdx) => `
                        <div class="bg-dark-950/90 border border-purple-500/35 rounded-2xl p-2.5 space-y-2">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between px-1"><span class="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-300">🔄 Kolo ${sIdx + 1} z ${totalSetsCount}</span><span class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">Odcvič A1 → hneď A2</span></div>
                            ${a1.ex.sets[sIdx] ? `<div class="space-y-1"><div class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-white px-1 flex items-center justify-between"><span><span class="text-purple-400">A1:</span> ${a1.ex.name}</span><button onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${a1.ex.exerciseId}')" class="text-[10px] text-sky-400">📘 Technika</button></div>${renderSingleSetCardHtml(a1.ex, a1.exIdx, a1.ex.sets[sIdx], sIdx, `A1 • ${sIdx + 1}`)}</div>` : ''}
                            ${a2.ex.sets[sIdx] ? `<div class="space-y-1 pt-1"><div class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-white px-1 flex items-center justify-between"><span><span class="text-amber-400">A2:</span> ${a2.ex.name}</span><button onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${a2.ex.exerciseId}')" class="text-[10px] text-sky-400">📘 Technika</button></div>${renderSingleSetCardHtml(a2.ex, a2.exIdx, a2.ex.sets[sIdx], sIdx, `A2 • ${sIdx + 1}`)}</div>` : ''}
                        </div>
                    `).join('');

                    return `
                        <div class="bg-dark-900 border-2 border-purple-500/70 rounded-3xl p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
                            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                                <div class="min-w-0"><span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40">⚡ SUPERSÉRIA (A1 + A2 SPOLU)</span><h3 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white mt-1.5">A1: ${a1.ex.name}</h3><h3 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-purple-200 mt-0.5">A2: ${a2.ex.name}</h3></div>
                                <button onclick="removeExerciseFromWorkout(${firstIdx})" class="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-dark-800 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 shrink-0">✕</button>
                            </div>
                            ${(lastPerf1 || lastPerf2) ? `<div class="bg-dark-950 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl p-2.5 text-[10.5px] space-y-0.5">${lastPerf1 ? `<div class="text-amber-400 font-bold">🏆 A1 naposledy: ${lastPerf1.headline}</div>` : ''}${lastPerf2 ? `<div class="text-amber-300 font-bold">🏆 A2 naposledy: ${lastPerf2.headline}</div>` : ''}</div>` : ''}
                            <div class="space-y-3">${roundsHtml}</div>
                            <button onclick="addSet(${firstIdx})" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/15 hover:bg-purple-500/25 border border-dashed border-purple-500/40 text-xs font-bold text-purple-200">+ Pridať ďalšie kolo supersérie (A1 + A2)</button>
                            <div class="pt-2 border-t border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                                <span class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-semibold">Blok ${bIdx + 1} z ${blocks.length}</span>
                                ${!isLastBlock ? `<button onclick="setExpandedExercise(${nextBlockFirstIdx})" class="bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs px-4 py-2 rounded-xl">Ďalší cvik →</button>` : `<div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button onclick="switchTab('database')" class="bg-dark-800 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 font-bold text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-xl">+ Cvik</button><button onclick="saveWorkoutToHistory()" class="bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl">Dokončiť ✓</button></div>`}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }

                const { ex, exIdx } = block.items[0];
                const catObj = CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === ex.category);
                const doneCount = ex.sets.filter(s => s.completed).length;
                const allDone = doneCount === ex.sets.length && ex.sets.length > 0;

                if (!isExpanded) {
                    const maxW = Math.max(...ex.sets.map(s => parseFloat(s.weight) || 0));
                    const best = maxW > 0 ? `max ${maxW} kg` : (ex.sets[0]?.mode === 'time' ? formatDuration(ex.sets[0].duration) : '');
                    return `<div onclick="setExpandedExercise(${exIdx})" class="cursor-pointer bg-dark-900 hover:bg-dark-850 border ${allDone ? 'border-brand-500/40' : 'border-zinc-800'} rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0"><div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold shrink-0 ${allDone ? 'bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950' : 'bg-dark-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-700'}">${allDone ? '✓' : bIdx + 1}</div><div class="min-w-0"><h4 class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">${ex.name}</h4><div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">${doneCount}/${ex.sets.length} sérií ${best ? `• <span class="text-amber-400">${best}</span>` : ''}</div></div></div><span class="text-xs font-bold text-brand-400 shrink-0">Otvoriť →</span></div>`;
                }

                const lastPerf = getLastPerformance(ex.exerciseId);
                const suggestedWarmupW = lastPerf?.maxWeight || parseFloat(ex.sets[0]?.weight) || 60;
                const setsRowsHtml = ex.sets.map((set, setIdx) => renderSingleSetCardHtml(ex, exIdx, set, setIdx)).join('');

                return `
                    <div class="bg-dark-900 border-2 border-brand-500/70 rounded-3xl p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
                        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                                <div onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${ex.exerciseId}')" class="cursor-pointer w-14 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-dark-950 border border-zinc-800 p-1 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">${getExerciseIllustrationSVG(ex.name, ex.category)}</div>
                                <div class="min-w-0"><span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-brand-400">${catObj?.name || ''}</span><h3 onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${ex.exerciseId}')" class="cursor-pointer text-base font-extrabold text-white leading-tight mt-0.5">${ex.name}</h3></div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                                <button onclick="openWarmupModal(${suggestedWarmupW})" class="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 text-[10px] font-extrabold">🔥 Rozohriať</button>
                                <button onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${ex.exerciseId}')" class="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-sky-500/15 border border-sky-500/30 text-sky-300 text-[10px] font-extrabold">📘 Graf & Info</button>
                                <button onclick="removeExerciseFromWorkout(${exIdx})" class="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-dark-800 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400">✕</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        ${lastPerf ? `<div class="bg-dark-950 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl p-2.5 flex items-start gap-2"><span class="text-sm">🏆</span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-amber-400">Naposledy (${lastPerf.date}): max ${lastPerf.headline}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">${lastPerf.setsText}</div></div></div>` : ''}
                        <div class="space-y-2.5">${setsRowsHtml}</div>
                        <button onclick="addSet(${exIdx})" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-dark-850 hover:bg-dark-800 border border-dashed border-zinc-700 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-300">+ Pridať ďalšiu sériu</button>
                        <div class="pt-2 border-t border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <span class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-semibold">Blok ${bIdx + 1} z ${blocks.length}</span>
                            ${!isLastBlock ? `<button onclick="setExpandedExercise(${nextBlockFirstIdx})" class="bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs px-4 py-2 rounded-xl">Ďalší cvik →</button>` : `<div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button onclick="switchTab('database')" class="bg-dark-800 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 font-bold text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-xl">+ Cvik</button><button onclick="saveWorkoutToHistory()" class="bg-brand-500 text-zinc-950 font-extrabold text-xs px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl">Dokončiť ✓</button></div>`}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function saveWorkoutToHistory() {
            if (activeWorkout.length === 0) return;
            stopRestTimer();
            const title = document.getElementById('workout-title').value.trim() || 'Tréning';
            const totalVolume = calculateTotalVolume(activeWorkout);
            const prof = getActiveProfile();
            const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('sk-SK', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
            const durationMin = workoutStartTime ? Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - workoutStartTime) / 60000)) : 0;
            const snapshotExercises = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(activeWorkout));

            const existingIdx = editingHistoryId ? workoutHistory.findIndex(w => w.id === editingHistoryId) : -1;
            if (existingIdx >= 0) {
                const prev = workoutHistory[existingIdx];
                workoutHistory[existingIdx] = { ...prev, title, totalVolume, durationMin: Math.max(prev.durationMin || 0, durationMin), readiness: { ...preWorkoutReadiness }, exercises: snapshotExercises };
            } else {
                workoutHistory.unshift({ id: 'workout-' + Date.now(), profileId: prof.id, profileName: prof.name, title, date: dateStr, durationMin, totalVolume, readiness: { ...preWorkoutReadiness }, exercises: snapshotExercises });
            }

            editingHistoryId = null; activeWorkout = []; expandedExerciseIndex = 0; workoutStartTime = null;
            localStorage.removeItem('fitlog_workout_start');
            document.getElementById('workout-title').value = 'Dnešný tréning';
            saveWorkoutMeta(); saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); renderDatabase(); renderHistory(); switchTab('history');
            sendCloudTesterLog('workout_completed', {
                title,
                date: dateStr,
                durationMin,
                totalVolume,
                exercisesCount: snapshotExercises.length,
                exercises: snapshotExercises
            });
            showToast(`Uložené! Nadvíhané: ${formatVolumeKg(totalVolume)}`);
        }

        function reopenWorkoutFromHistory(workoutId) {
            const w = workoutHistory.find(x => x.id === workoutId);
            if (!w) return;
            editingHistoryId = w.id;
            activeWorkout = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w.exercises || []));
            expandedExerciseIndex = Math.max(0, activeWorkout.length - 1);
            workoutStartTime = Date.now() - ((w.durationMin || 0) * 60000);
            localStorage.setItem('fitlog_workout_start', workoutStartTime.toString());
            document.getElementById('workout-title').value = w.title || 'Tréning';
            if (w.readiness) { preWorkoutReadiness = { ...w.readiness }; initPreWorkoutReadinessUI(); }
            saveWorkoutMeta(); saveState(); renderActiveWorkout(); switchAppDomain('training'); switchTab('workout');
            showToast('Tréning znovu otvorený – môžeš upravovať alebo pridať cviky');
        }

        function shareWorkoutReport(workoutId) {
            const w = workoutHistory.find(x => x.id === workoutId);
            if (!w) return;
            const vol = w.totalVolume !== undefined ? w.totalVolume : calculateTotalVolume(w.exercises || []);
            let report = `MM LAB – Tréningový Report\n👤 Meno: ${w.profileName || getActiveProfile().name}\n📅 Dátum: ${w.date} | 🏋️ Nadvíhané: ${formatVolumeKg(vol)}\n📋 Tréning: ${w.title}\n-------------------------------------\n`;
            (w.exercises || []).forEach((ex, i) => {
                const setsStr = (ex.sets || []).map(s => `${s.weight ? s.weight + 'kg' : 'BW'}×${s.mode === 'time' ? formatDuration(s.duration) : s.reps + 'x'} (RIR ${s.rir})`).join(', ');
                report += `${i + 1}. ${ex.name}: ${setsStr}\n`;
            });
            copyTextToClipboard(report, 'Report tréningu skopírovaný do schránky!');
        }

        function deleteHistoryRecord(workoutId) { workoutHistory = workoutHistory.filter(w => w.id !== workoutId); saveState(); renderHistory(); renderDatabase(); }

        function renderHistory() {
            const container = document.getElementById('history-list'), badge = document.getElementById('history-count-badge');
            if (!container) return;
            const list = workoutHistory.filter(w => (w.profileId || 'me') === activeProfileId);
            badge.textContent = `${list.length} záznamov`;
            if (list.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-10 px-4 bg-dark-900/60 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl text-xs text-zinc-400">Zatiaľ žiadne uložené tréningy pre tento profil.</div>`; return; }
            container.innerHTML = list.map(w => {
                const vol = w.totalVolume !== undefined ? w.totalVolume : calculateTotalVolume(w.exercises || []);
                return `
                    <div class="bg-dark-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
                        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <div>
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap"><span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400">${w.date}</span>${vol > 0 ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">🏋️ ${formatVolumeKg(vol)}</span>` : ''}${w.durationMin ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold bg-dark-800 text-zinc-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">⏱ ${w.durationMin} min</span>` : ''}</div>
                                <h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-white mt-1">${w.title}</h3>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 flex-wrap justify-end">
                                <button onclick="reopenWorkoutFromHistory('${w.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/35 text-amber-300 text-[11px] font-extrabold">✏️ Pokračovať</button>
                                <button onclick="exportWorkoutToTCX('${w.id}')" class="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-sky-500/15 border border-sky-500/30 text-sky-300 text-[11px] font-extrabold">⌚ .TCX</button>
                                <button onclick="shareWorkoutReport('${w.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-500/15 border border-brand-500/30 text-brand-400 text-[11px] font-extrabold">📤 Report</button>
                                <button onclick="deleteHistoryRecord('${w.id}')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-dark-800 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400">✕</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="space-y-1.5">
                            ${(w.exercises || []).map(ex => `<div onclick="openExerciseDetailModal('${ex.exerciseId}')" class="cursor-pointer bg-dark-850 hover:bg-dark-800 border border-zinc-800/80 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">${ex.name}</div><div class="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">${(ex.sets || []).map(s => `${s.weight ? s.weight + ' kg' : 'BW'} × ${s.mode === 'time' ? formatDuration(s.duration) : s.reps + '×'} (RIR ${s.rir})`).join(' | ')}</div></div><span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-sky-400 shrink-0">📈 Graf</span></div>`).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function openBackupModal() { document.getElementById('modal-backup').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        function closeBackupModal() { document.getElementById('modal-backup').classList.add('hidden'); }
        function exportDataJSON() {
            const payload = { app: 'MM LAB (MiroMedo_fit)', version: APP_VERSION, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), profiles, exercises, splits: savedSplits, history: workoutHistory, supplements, userSupplementStacks, suppTakenByDate, nutritionDataByProfile, mealPresets, bodyCheckInsByProfile, weeklyCheckInsByProfile, biometricsByProfile };
            const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = `MM_LAB_zaloha_${getTodayISO()}.json`; a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url); closeBackupModal(); showToast('Záloha stiahnutá');
        }
        function importDataJSON(event) {
            const file = event.target.files?.[0];
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (e) {
                try {
                    const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
                    if (Array.isArray(data.profiles)) profiles = data.profiles;
                    if (Array.isArray(data.exercises)) exercises = data.exercises;
                    if (Array.isArray(data.splits)) savedSplits = data.splits;
                    if (Array.isArray(data.history)) workoutHistory = data.history;
                    if (Array.isArray(data.supplements)) supplements = data.supplements;
                    if (data.userSupplementStacks) userSupplementStacks = data.userSupplementStacks;
                    if (data.suppTakenByDate) suppTakenByDate = data.suppTakenByDate;
                    if (data.nutritionDataByProfile) nutritionDataByProfile = data.nutritionDataByProfile;
                    if (Array.isArray(data.mealPresets)) mealPresets = data.mealPresets;
                    if (data.bodyCheckInsByProfile) bodyCheckInsByProfile = data.bodyCheckInsByProfile;
                    if (data.weeklyCheckInsByProfile) weeklyCheckInsByProfile = data.weeklyCheckInsByProfile;
                    if (data.biometricsByProfile) biometricsByProfile = data.biometricsByProfile;
                    saveState(); updateHeaderProfileUI(); initBiometricsUI(); renderDatabase(); renderSplits(); renderHistory(); renderNutritionSection(); renderSupplementsSection();
                    closeBackupModal(); showToast('Dáta úspešne obnovené zo zálohy!');
                } catch (err) { showToast('Neplatný záložný súbor .JSON'); }
            };
            reader.readAsText(file);
        }

        function copyTextToClipboard(text, successMsg) {
            const ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
            try { document.execCommand('copy'); showToast(successMsg); } catch (e) { showToast('Nepodarilo sa skopírovať'); }
            document.body.removeChild(ta);
        }

        let toastTimeout = null;
        function showToast(msg) {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast'), msgEl = document.getElementById('toast-message');
            if (!toast || !msgEl) return;
            msgEl.textContent = msg;
            toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2');
            toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
            if (toastTimeout) clearTimeout(toastTimeout);
            toastTimeout = setTimeout(() => { toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0'); toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2'); }, 2600);
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const savedTitle = localStorage.getItem('fitlog_workout_title');
            if (savedTitle) document.getElementById('workout-title').value = savedTitle;
            const newExCat = document.getElementById('new-ex-category');
            if (newExCat) newExCat.innerHTML = CATEGORIES.filter(c => c.id !== 'all').map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.name}</option>`).join('');
            const techSel = document.getElementById('builder-technique-select');
            if (techSel) techSel.innerHTML = SET_TECHNIQUES.map(t => `<option value="${t.id}">${t.label}</option>`).join('');

            updateHeaderProfileUI(); initBiometricsUI(); initPreWorkoutReadinessUI(); updateSuppBadgeCount();
            renderCategoryPills(); renderDatabase(); renderSplits(); renderActiveWorkout(); renderHistory(); startWorkoutTimer();
            checkTesterAccessOnStartup().then(() => {
                renderSplits();
                fetchSharedTrainerSplits(false);
                fetchSharedTrainerMealPlans(false);
                fetchScheduledWorkouts(false);
                fetchLabFeedPosts();
                fetchCommunityFoodCatalog();
            });
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
